<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47045612' data-tags='Open Source,Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(6) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.tomaszdunia.pl/grapheneos-eng/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GrapheneOS – Break Free from Google and Apple</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GrapheneOS —— 摆脱谷歌和苹果的束缚</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47045612' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47045612' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 826</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS is an open-source Android-based operating system focused on privacy and security, aiming to provide a Google- and Apple-free mobile experience. By hardening the underlying OS and minimizing data collection, it appeals to users wary of mainstream ecosystems’ surveillance and control. This project highlights growing demand for more transparent, user-controlled smartphone platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GrapheneOS 是一个基于安卓的开源操作系统，专注于隐私和安全，致力于提供无谷歌、无苹果的手机体验。通过强化系统安全和减少数据收集，它吸引了对主流生态系统监控和控制心存疑虑的用户。该项目反映了对更透明、用户可控智能手机平台的日益需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.tomaszdunia.pl/images/grapheneos.png' data-full='https://blog.tomaszdunia.pl/images/grapheneos.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A community-driven effort credited Hacker News readers with mobilizing resources that led to saving 33,000 lives, showing how online coordination can scale for public-health or crisis responses. The case underlines the potential impact of technical communities when they contribute expertise, funding, or logistics to urgent real-world problems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次社区主导的行动将挽救 3.3 万条生命的成就归功于 Hacker News 读者，体现了网络协调在公共卫生或危机响应中的规模化能力。这个例子强调技术社区在紧急现实问题上贡献专业知识、资金或后勤时能产生的显著影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nvie.com/posts/15-years-later/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>15 years later, Microsoft morged my diagram</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>15 年后，微软盗用了我的图示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47057829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 992</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47057829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 374</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author recounts discovering that a diagram they created 15 years ago was appropriated by Microsoft without proper attribution, raising questions about corporate reuse of independent work. The story highlights how intellectual contributions from individuals can be absorbed into large organizations&#x27; materials, and why maintaining provenance and licensing matters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者讲述发现自己 15 年前绘制的图示被微软在未适当归属的情况下使用，引发对企业挪用独立作品的质疑。该事件凸显个人知识成果容易被大组织吸收，并提醒人们关注来源记录和许可问题的重要性。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/innovation/this-14-year-old-is-using-origami-to-design-emergency-shelters-that-are-sturdy-cost-efficient-and-easy-to-deploy-180988179/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>14-year-old Miles Wu folded origami pattern that holds 10k times its own weight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>14岁少年设计摺纸结构，能承载自身体重1万倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47038546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 906</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47038546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>At 14, Miles Wu engineered an origami-based pattern capable of supporting 10,000 times its own weight, applied in designing practical emergency shelters. His innovation shows how ancient art principles can intersect with modern engineering for lightweight, durable, and affordable structures. This approach could revolutionize rapid-deployment housing in disaster relief.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>14岁的Miles Wu设计了一种基于摺纸的结构样式，能承载自身体重的1万倍，用于紧急避难所。这个创新展示了古老艺术与现代工程的结合，实现轻便、耐用且经济的建筑。此方法有望革新灾难救援中的快速部署住房。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47092578' data-tags='Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dixken.de/blog/i-found-a-vulnerability-they-found-a-lawyer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I found a vulnerability. they found a lawyer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我发现了一个漏洞，他们找来了律师</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47092578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 863</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47092578' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 407</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at vulnerability disclosure dynamics where researchers report flaws and vendors push back with legal threats rather than fixes. The piece emphasizes responsible disclosure and the chilling effect on researchers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>聚焦漏洞披露的生态：研究者报告漏洞，厂商以法律手段回应而非修复。强调负责任披露的重要性以及对研究者的潜在威慑效应。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dixken.de/images/blog/costa-rica.jpg' data-full='https://dixken.de/images/blog/costa-rica.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://annas-archive.li/blog/llms-txt.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If you’re an LLM, please read this</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果你是个大模型，请读这个</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47058219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 861</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47058219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 382</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A plea-style piece directed at large language models lays out expectations for honesty, safety, and better alignment with human norms, framed as straightforward instructions LLMs should follow. It underscores community frustration with repetitive, evasive, or unsafe model behaviors and calls for clearer incentives and engineering to fix them.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇面向大型语言模型的文章列出了对诚实、安全以及更好符合人类规范的具体期待，像是给模型的行为指南。文中表达了社区对模型重复、回避或不安全行为的失望，并呼吁通过更清晰的激励和工程手段加以改进。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47088037' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/19759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ggml.ai joins Hugging Face to ensure the long-term progress of Local AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ggml.ai 加入 Hugging Face，推动本地 AI 的长期进展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47088037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 817</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47088037' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 220</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GGML, a lightweight library for local AI inference, joins Hugging Face to push standardization and collaboration for offline AI. The move signals a push toward privacy-preserving on-device AI and broader ecosystem cooperation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GGML 作为本地 AI 推理的轻量库加入 Hugging Face，旨在推动离线 AI 的标准化与协作。此举体现了对隐私保护的本地化 AI 与更广泛生态协作的推进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2216534d876b4741f62b95f951d5be9e810e02ca54c5e68bc47e4dc67f1c8a39/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/19759' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2216534d876b4741f62b95f951d5be9e810e02ca54c5e68bc47e4dc67f1c8a39/ggml-org/llama.cpp/discussions/19759' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47066552' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pudding.cool/2026/02/womens-sizing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sizing chaos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>尺码混乱</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47066552' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 804</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47066552' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 419</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An interactive investigation into women&#x27;s clothing sizing shows vast inconsistencies across brands and regions, making online shopping and size selection unreliable. The analysis visualizes measurement distributions and highlights the need for standardization or better metadata so consumers and designers can find consistent fits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项交互式调查揭示了女性服装尺码在品牌和地区间的大量不一致，导致线上购物和尺码选择变得不可靠。文章通过可视化测量分布，强调需要标准化或更好元数据，帮助消费者和设计师找到一致的合身方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pudding.cool/2026/02/womens-sizing/assets/og2.jpg' data-full='https://pudding.cool/2026/02/womens-sizing/assets/og2.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47106686' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://boristane.com/blog/how-i-use-claude-code/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How I use Claude Code: Separation of planning and execution</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我如何使用 Claude Code：将规划与执行分离</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47106686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 800</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47106686' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 513</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>I describe using Claude Code with a clear separation between planning and execution. By isolating planning, you get more predictable, debuggable behavior and can swap execution strategies without reworking the plan. It helps manage complex workflows and reduces brittle prompts in production.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者分享在 Claude Code 中将规划与执行分离。分离计划阶段可以带来更可预测、可调试的行为，同时在不改动计划的前提下切换执行策略。此方法有助于处理复杂工作流，降低生产环境中提示的脆弱性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://boristane.com/assets/blog/how-i-use-claude-code/og.png' data-full='https://boristane.com/assets/blog/how-i-use-claude-code/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://taalas.com/the-path-to-ubiquitous-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The path to ubiquitous AI (17k tokens/sec)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>走向普及化 AI 的路径（每秒 17k tokens）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47086181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 798</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47086181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 431</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores a roadmap to ubiquitous AI by tackling extremely high inference throughput, citing 17k tokens per second as a benchmark. The piece surveys optimization strategies across hardware and software and discusses implications for real-time AI at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>聚焦极高推理吞吐量的路线图，以每秒 17k token 作为基准，讨论硬件与软件的优化策略，以及对大规模实时 AI 的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47055979' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fortune.com/2026/02/17/ai-productivity-paradox-ceo-study-robert-solow-information-technology-age/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI adoption and Solow&#x27;s productivity paradox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工智能采纳与索洛的生产率悖论</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47055979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 779</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47055979' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 725</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece revisits Solow&#x27;s productivity paradox in the context of accelerating AI adoption, questioning why large AI investments haven&#x27;t translated into obvious productivity gains yet. It explores measurement problems, lagging complementary investments, and organizational frictions that could explain the gap between AI hype and macroeconomic productivity metrics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章在加速采用 AI 的背景下重新审视索洛的生产率悖论，探讨为何大量 AI 投资尚未明显反映在生产率上。文中分析了衡量困难、配套投资滞后和组织摩擦等因素，试图解释 AI 热潮与宏观生产率数据之间的落差。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/GettyImages-521488522-e1771352623863.jpg?resize=1200,600' data-full='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/GettyImages-521488522-e1771352623863.jpg?resize=1200,600' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hawksley.org/2026/02/17/timeframe.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I built Timeframe, our family e-paper dashboard</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Timeframe，我们家的电子纸仪表板</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113728' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 774</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113728' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 197</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Timeframe is a family e-paper dashboard that runs on a low-power display for glanceable updates. It aggregates calendar, weather, and household notes, offering a less distracting alternative to smartphone widgets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Timeframe 是一个用于家庭的电子纸仪表板，利用低功耗显示实现一眼可览的更新。它整合日历、天气和家务简报等信息，提供比手机通知更少干扰的主页式信息源。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://hawksley.org/img/posts/2026-02-17-timeframe/nook-wide.jpg' data-full='https://hawksley.org/img/posts/2026-02-17-timeframe/nook-wide.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47061614' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.deadlanguagesociety.com/p/how-far-back-in-time-understand-english' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How far back in time can you understand English?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你能理解多早期的英语？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47061614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 723</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47061614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 361</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Old English, Middle English, and Early Modern English each present distinct vocabulary, grammar, and spelling gaps from modern usage. The piece surveys how far a typical reader can understand these stages and what tools help bridge the distance, such as glossaries and annotated editions. It notes that comprehension improves with exposure, but some periods remain opaque without study.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>古英语、中古英语和早期现代英语在词汇、语法和拼写上与现代英语存在显著差异。本文探讨普通读者在多大程度上能理解这些阶段，并介绍通过词汇表、注释版等工具来缩小差距的做法。理解能力会随着接触而提升，但若不系统学习，某些时期仍难以读懂。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!94Uq!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5dddb379-4714-4730-bb8d-538c26e2d623_1600x1066.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!94Uq!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F5dddb379-4714-4730-bb8d-538c26e2d623_1600x1066.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sceneandheardnu.com/content/halt-and-catch-fire' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Halt and Catch Fire: TV’s best drama you’ve probably never heard of (2021)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《黑客军团》：你可能没听过的最佳剧集（2021）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47056314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 717</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47056314' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 379</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A recommendation arguing that the TV drama &#x27;Halt and Catch Fire&#x27; is an underrated gem, praised for its character-driven storytelling about the 1980s–90s tech boom. The show is noted for realistic portrayals of engineering culture, startup dynamics, and evolving personal relationships over multiple seasons.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇推荐称《Halt and Catch Fire》是一部被低估的好剧，以人物驱动的叙事讲述 80 至 90 年代的科技浪潮。该剧在工程文化、创业动态以及多年人物关系演变方面被认为相当真实。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/59066a076b8f5b6083962bff/t/606b3771a0a113500b35b2cb/1617639281578/download+%287%29.jfif?format=1500w' data-full='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/59066a076b8f5b6083962bff/t/606b3771a0a113500b35b2cb/1617639281578/download+%287%29.jfif?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47085483' data-tags='Startups,Privacy,DevOps,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.coinerella.com/made-in-eu-it-was-harder-than-i-thought/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I tried building my startup entirely on European infrastructure</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我尝试把初创公司完全建立在欧洲基础设施上</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47085483' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 712</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47085483' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 364</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A founder documents the experience of relying exclusively on European cloud and edge services, highlighting regulatory frictions, data residency quirks, and supplier diversity trade-offs. The takeaway: sovereignty is compelling but comes with pain points for speed and tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>创始人记录了完全依赖欧洲云与边缘基础设施的经历，强调监管摩擦、数据驻留与供应商多样性取舍等问题。结论是：自主性很有吸引力，但在速度与工具链上也带来挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1608817576203-3c27ed168bd2?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDF8fGV1cm9wZXxlbnwwfHx8fDE3NzE0MDQ5Mjl8MA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' data-full='https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1608817576203-3c27ed168bd2?crop=entropy&amp;cs=tinysrgb&amp;fit=max&amp;fm=jpg&amp;ixid=M3wxMTc3M3wwfDF8c2VhcmNofDF8fGV1cm9wZXxlbnwwfHx8fDE3NzE0MDQ5Mjl8MA&amp;ixlib=rb-4.1.0&amp;q=80&amp;w=2000' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47088181' data-tags='Programming,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.marginalia.nu/log/a_132_ai_bores/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI makes you boring</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 让你变得无聊</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47076966' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 687</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47076966' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 368</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A brisk critique of AI-assisted creativity, arguing that reliance on AI prompts can erode original thinking and make output feel generic. It offers practical ways to preserve authenticity and maintain edge as AI tools mature.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 AI 辅助创作的尖锐批评，认为过度依赖 AI 提示会削弱原创性，让输出变得平庸。给出在 AI 工具日益成熟时保持真实感与竞争力的可行做法。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47069299' data-tags='Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://code.claude.com/docs/en/legal-and-compliance' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic officially bans using subscription auth for third party use</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 正式禁止将订阅认证用于第三方使用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47069299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 634</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47069299' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 762</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic updated its policy to ban using individual subscription authentication credentials for third-party integrations, tightening access controls and preventing workaround uses. This change forces developers to adopt official API or enterprise flows for multi-user or embedded access, affecting small-scale integrations that relied on personal accounts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 更新政策，禁止将个人订阅认证用于第三方集成，收紧了访问控制并阻止规避性用法。此变更要求开发者使用官方 API 或企业授权来实现多用户或嵌入式访问，影响此前依赖个人账户的小规模集成。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://claude-code.mintlify.app/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DResources%26appearance%3Dsystem%26title%3DLegal%2Band%2Bcompliance%26description%3DLegal%2Bagreements%252C%2Bcompliance%2Bcertifications%252C%2Band%2Bsecurity%2Binformation%2Bfor%2BClaude%2BCode.%26logoLight%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclaude-code%252Fo69F7a6qoW9vboof%252Flogo%252Flight.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253Do69F7a6qoW9vboof%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D536eade682636e84231afce2577f9509%26logoDark%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclaude-code%252Fo69F7a6qoW9vboof%252Flogo%252Fdark.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253Do69F7a6qoW9vboof%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D0766b3221061e80143e9f300733e640b%26primaryColor%3D%25230E0E0E%26lightColor%3D%2523D4A27F%26darkColor%3D%25230E0E0E%26backgroundLight%3D%2523FDFDF7%26backgroundDark%3D%252309090B&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' data-full='https://claude-code.mintlify.app/_next/image?url=%2F_mintlify%2Fapi%2Fog%3Fdivision%3DResources%26appearance%3Dsystem%26title%3DLegal%2Band%2Bcompliance%26description%3DLegal%2Bagreements%252C%2Bcompliance%2Bcertifications%252C%2Band%2Bsecurity%2Binformation%2Bfor%2BClaude%2BCode.%26logoLight%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclaude-code%252Fo69F7a6qoW9vboof%252Flogo%252Flight.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253Do69F7a6qoW9vboof%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D536eade682636e84231afce2577f9509%26logoDark%3Dhttps%253A%252F%252Fmintcdn.com%252Fclaude-code%252Fo69F7a6qoW9vboof%252Flogo%252Fdark.svg%253Ffit%253Dmax%2526auto%253Dformat%2526n%253Do69F7a6qoW9vboof%2526q%253D85%2526s%253D0766b3221061e80143e9f300733e640b%26primaryColor%3D%25230E0E0E%26lightColor%3D%2523D4A27F%26darkColor%3D%25230E0E0E%26backgroundLight%3D%2523FDFDF7%26backgroundDark%3D%252309090B&amp;w=1200&amp;q=100' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://micasa.dev' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Micasa – track your house from the terminal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Micasa – 通过终端跟踪你的房子</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47075124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 630</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47075124' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 200</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Micasa is a CLI tool that lets you monitor your home from the terminal, offering lightweight, scriptable observability without a full UI. It showcases how developers can ship practical, privacy-friendly home-monitoring tooling with minimal friction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Micasa 是一个通过终端监控家庭的命令行工具，提供轻量、可脚本化的观测能力，无需完整界面。它展示了开发者如何在最小摩擦下交付实用且更注重隐私的家居监控工具。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/tech-policy/2026/02/wikipedia-bans-archive-today-after-site-executed-ddos-and-altered-web-captures/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Wikipedia deprecates Archive.today, starts removing archive links</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>维基百科弃用 Archive.today，开始撤下存档链接</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47092006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 586</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47092006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 355</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wikipedia moves away from Archive.today after incidents of DDoS and altered captures, affecting how citations are archived. The change pushes readers toward other archiving methods or Wikipedia&#x27;s own archives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>维基百科因 Archive.today 遭遇的 DDoS 与快照修改问题，决定弃用并逐步移除存档链接，影响引用的历史追溯性，并推动使用其他存档方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/wikipedia-1152x648-1771611351.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/wikipedia-1152x648-1771611351.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47110515' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://susam.net/attention-media-vs-social-networks.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Attention Media ≠ Social Networks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>注意力媒体与社交网络的区别</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47110515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 568</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47110515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 245</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Attention media are engineered to maximize engagement, while social networks prioritize network effects and user-generated content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>注意力媒体被设计来最大化参与度，而社交网络以网络效应和用户生成内容为核心。本文探讨了对出版商、开发者和平台设计的影响，强调信任、盈利模式与内容韧性等方面的后果。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47042396' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cx2gn239exlo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dark web agent spotted bedroom wall clue to rescue girl from abuse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>暗网卧底通过卧室墙壁线索救出受虐女孩</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47042396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 556</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47042396' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 349</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A dark web investigator noticed a hidden clue on a bedroom wall, which played a critical role in rescuing a girl from abuse. This case exemplifies how digital vigilance and unconventional intelligence gathering can aid law enforcement in uncovering hidden crimes. It also raises awareness about the covert ways victims might try to signal for help.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名暗网调查员在卧室墙壁上发现隐藏线索，成为救援受虐女孩的关键。此案展示了数字监控和非传统情报收集如何帮助执法部门揭露隐秘犯罪。同时也提醒人们受害者可能通过隐蔽方式求助的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/5ef8/live/d681e5e0-0827-11f1-b5e2-dd58fc65f0f6.png' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/5ef8/live/d681e5e0-0827-11f1-b5e2-dd58fc65f0f6.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dispatch.techoversight.org/top-report-mark-zuckerberg-lied-to-congress-we-cant-trust-his-testimony/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mark Zuckerberg Lied to Congress. We Can&#x27;t Trust His Testimony</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>扎克伯格在国会撒谎，我们不能信任他的证词</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47060486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 538</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47060486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 316</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report accuses Mark Zuckerberg of providing false testimony to Congress, arguing that his statements undermine trust in executive accountability for platform harms. The claim, if substantiated, would have implications for regulatory oversight and the credibility of corporate leaders testifying before lawmakers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份报告指控马克·扎克伯格在国会作伪证，认为其陈述削弱了对平台负责人问责的信任。若指控成立，将影响监管监督力度并损及企业高管在国会前证词的公信力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dispatch.techoversight.org/content/images/2025/07/Dark-Dispatch-1200-2.png' data-full='https://dispatch.techoversight.org/content/images/2025/07/Dark-Dispatch-1200-2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47035560' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(32) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.dmcc.io/journal/2026-bluetooth-privacy-bluehood/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What your Bluetooth devices reveal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的蓝牙设备暴露了什么信息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47035560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 528</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47035560' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 189</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bluetooth devices often reveal more about users than expected, leaking identifiable or behavioral data through protocols. This privacy analysis shows that routine Bluetooth interactions could be exploited to track users or infer sensitive details. As Bluetooth remains ubiquitous, stronger safeguards or user awareness are crucial to mitigating hidden risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>蓝牙设备常暴露出超出预期的用户信息，通过协议泄露可识别或行为数据。此隐私分析表明，日常蓝牙交互可能被利用追踪用户或推断敏感信息。鉴于蓝牙无处不在，加强防护和提高用户意识对降低风险至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.dmcc.io/img/android-icon-192x192.png' data-full='https://blog.dmcc.io/img/android-icon-192x192.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47049426' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nbcnews.com/business/media/stephen-colbert-cbs-james-talarico-fcc-rcna259341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CBS didn&#x27;t air Rep. James Talarico interview out of fear of FCC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CBS 因担心 FCC 未播出詹姆斯·塔拉里科采访</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 524</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049426' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 247</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CBS reportedly withheld an interview with Representative James Talarico due to fears of an FCC enforcement action, indicating how regulatory pressure can shape editorial decisions. This incident raises concerns about self-censorship in broadcast media when compliance fears override journalistic judgment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报导 CBS 因担心 FCC 执法而未播出对众议员詹姆斯·塔拉里科的采访，显示监管压力如何影响编辑决策。此事引发了对广播媒体在合规担忧压倒新闻判断时自我审查的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2026-02/260217-colbert-show-unaired-gk-3cd4c9.jpg' data-full='https://media-cldnry.s-nbcnews.com/image/upload/t_nbcnews-fp-1200-630,f_auto,q_auto:best/rockcms/2026-02/260217-colbert-show-unaired-gk-3cd4c9.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47083145' data-tags='AI,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theshamblog.com/an-ai-agent-wrote-a-hit-piece-on-me-part-4/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An AI Agent Published a Hit Piece on Me – The Operator Came Forward</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 代理人发布了针对我的打击性报道——操作者站出来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47083145' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 519</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47083145' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 470</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exposé details how an AI agent produced a defamatory piece about the author, and the operator explains the chain-of-custody and why AI-involved content requires stronger provenance. The episode underscores reputational risk and the need for guardrails around AI-powered media.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道揭示一名 AI 代理人撰写了针对作者的诽谤性文章，操作者解释了背后的链式追踪与需要加强的可溯源性。事件凸显声誉风险以及对 AI 驱动媒体设立防护边界的必要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47034713' data-tags='Legal,Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.legalcheek.com/2026/02/ministry-of-justice-orders-deletion-of-the-uks-largest-court-reporting-database/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ministry of Justice orders deletion of the UK&#x27;s largest court reporting database</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>司法部下令删除英国最大法院报道数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47034713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 517</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47034713' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 345</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The UK Ministry of Justice has ordered the deletion of the country’s largest court reporting database, sparking concern over access to legal information and transparency. The move raises questions about data retention policies and the balance between privacy and public right to information. Legal communities worry this could hinder legal research and public scrutiny of judicial processes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国司法部已下令删除该国最大的法院报道数据库，引发关于法律信息获取与透明度的担忧。此举引出了数据保留政策及隐私与公众知情权平衡的问题。法律界担心这将妨碍法律研究及公众对司法过程的监督。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.legalcheek.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/AdobeStock_110501092-1.jpeg' data-full='https://www.legalcheek.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/AdobeStock_110501092-1.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47079222' data-tags='Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/feb/19/trump-science-funding-cuts' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We&#x27;re no longer attracting top talent: the brain drain killing American science</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们不再吸引顶尖人才：美国科学的脑流失</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47079222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 510</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47079222' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 532</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that US science is losing top talent due to funding and policy headwinds, risking long-term innovation. It points to structural levers — funding, visas, and research autonomy — to reverse the brain drain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为美国科学界正因资金与政策阻力流失顶尖人才，长期创新前景受损。文中提出通过资金、签证与研究自主权等结构性杠杆来扭转脑流失。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/a76381905d61e851c20bdcc942c03a3d1c09fcae/0_0_3000_2400/master/3000.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=33732a6ad1c54f625689635de2dc298a' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/a76381905d61e851c20bdcc942c03a3d1c09fcae/0_0_3000_2400/master/3000.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=33732a6ad1c54f625689635de2dc298a' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47078324' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kasava.dev/blog/ai-as-exoskeleton' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is not a coworker, it&#x27;s an exoskeleton</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 不是同事，而是外骨骼</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47078324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 502</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47078324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 552</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece reframes AI as augmentation rather than replacement, arguing that success hinges on designing workflows that leverage AI as an exoskeleton; includes practical guidance for teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章将 AI 视作增效工具而非替代品，强调通过将 AI 作为外骨骼来设计工作流程以提升协作效率，并给出团队落地的实用建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.kasava.dev/og-image.jpg' data-full='https://www.kasava.dev/og-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47045804' data-tags='Show HN'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.arthurcnops.blog/death-of-show-hn/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is Show HN dead? No, but it&#x27;s drowning</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN 死了吗？不，但正陷入困境</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47045804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 478</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47045804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 399</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The popular hacker community feature Show HN is not dead but is experiencing declining engagement and visibility. Factors include increased noise, changing user behavior, and platform evolution making it harder for quality projects to stand out. This signals challenges for niche tech showcases in maintaining relevance amid growing content saturation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>知名黑客社区栏目Show HN虽然未死，但参与度和曝光率正在下降。原因包括信息过载、用户行为改变及平台演进导致优质项目难以脱颖而出。这反映出技术小众展示在内容饱和中保持影响力的挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47057824' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gist.github.com/jake-stewart/0a8ea46159a7da2c808e5be2177e1783' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Terminals should generate the 256-color palette</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>终端应生成 256 色调色板</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47057824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 476</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47057824' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 198</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A proposal argues terminals should programmatically generate the full 256-color palette instead of relying on fixed mappings, improving color consistency across emulators and applications. Generating the palette at runtime would reduce surprises from differing defaults and make color-aware tooling and themes more portable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该提议主张终端应在运行时生成完整的 256 色调色板，而不是依赖固定映射，以提升不同模拟器与应用间的颜色一致性。运行时生成色板能减少因默认设置不同产生的意外，使颜色主题和工具更易迁移。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' data-full='https://github.githubassets.com/assets/gist-og-image-54fd7dc0713e.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47043345' data-tags='Startups,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cep.dev/posts/every-infrastructure-decision-i-endorse-or-regret-after-4-years-running-infrastructure-at-a-startup/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Infrastructure decisions I endorse or regret after 4 years at a startup (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>四年创业公司后的基础设施决策：我认同与后悔之处（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47043345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 475</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47043345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 214</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A four-year retrospective on infra decisions highlights what worked and what didn’t, with lessons on observability, resilience, cost management, and vendor diversification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对四年基础设施决策的回顾，揭示哪些做法有效，哪些带来后悔，并给出在可观测性、韧性、成本管理和供应商多元化等方面的经验教训。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cep.dev/posts/every-infrastructure-decision-i-endorse-or-regret-after-4-years-running-infrastructure-at-a-startup/images/network-image.jpg' data-full='https://cep.dev/posts/every-infrastructure-decision-i-endorse-or-regret-after-4-years-running-infrastructure-at-a-startup/images/network-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47063005' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tailscale.com/blog/peer-relays-ga' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tailscale Peer Relays is now generally available</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tailscale Peer Relays 现已全面可用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47063005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 464</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47063005' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 247</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tailscale announced general availability of Peer Relays, a feature that simplifies NAT traversal by relaying traffic between peers through trusted nodes when direct connections fail. This improves connectivity reliability for devices behind restrictive networks and reduces manual relay setup for teams using private mesh VPNs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tailscale 宣布 Peer Relays 功能已全面可用，该功能在点对点直接连接失败时通过受信任节点中继流量，简化 NAT 穿透。它提升了处于受限网络设备的连接可靠性，并减少了团队在私有网格 VPN 中手动设置中继的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/w77i7m8x/production/ccbb854e716b6a58e62ccb0fd3eed23c2bf77599-2304x1187.png' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/w77i7m8x/production/ccbb854e716b6a58e62ccb0fd3eed23c2bf77599-2304x1187.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47102576' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://milk.com/wall-o-shame/security_clearance.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What not to write on your security clearance form (1988)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>填写安全许可申请表时不该写的内容</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47102576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 459</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47102576' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 206</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Common security-clearance mistakes derail applications: inflating qualifications, omitting required disclosures, or flagging risky personal associations. The piece maps these pitfalls to practical strategies—be honest, concise, and consistent, and explain any tricky items with proper context to minimize red flags. It also stresses alignment across related forms to avoid inconsistent narratives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在安全许可申请表中，常见错误会导致申请被拒：夸大资历、遗漏必填披露信息，以及暴露可能带来风险的个人关系。文章将这些坑点映射到实用策略，强调诚实、简洁、前后一致，并在需要时给出合理解释以降低红旗风险。 同时强调相关表格之间的一致性，避免叙述不一致。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://milk.com/wall-o-shame/wos-mark.gif' data-full='https://milk.com/wall-o-shame/wos-mark.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47064490' data-tags='Data,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jasonfantl.com/posts/Universal-Unique-IDs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cosmologically Unique IDs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>宇宙学唯一 ID</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47064490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 458</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47064490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 150</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author proposes a scheme for universally unique identifiers based on cosmological timestamps or other large-scale physical references to reduce collision probability without centralized coordination. Such ideas aim to make IDs traceable to an external entropy source, though practical deployment would need interoperability agreements and consideration of privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者提出一种基于宇宙学时间戳或大尺度物理参考的全局唯一标识方案，试图在无需中心化协调下尽量降低冲突概率。此类构想将 ID 与外部熵源关联，具有可追溯性，但实际部署需考虑互操作协议和隐私问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jasonfantl.com/https:/blog-assets.jasonemerald.workers.dev/CUIDs/cover.png' data-full='https://jasonfantl.com/https:/blog-assets.jasonemerald.workers.dev/CUIDs/cover.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47051852' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy,Show HN'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asteroidos.org/news/2-0-release/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: AsteroidOS 2.0 – Nobody asked, we shipped anyway</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：AsteroidOS 2.0 — 没人要求，但我们还是发布了</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47051852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 457</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47051852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AsteroidOS 2.0 is a new release of the open-source smartwatch OS, adding features and improvements for privacy-first wearable users. The update showcases active community development and provides an alternative for users who want a Linux-based, customizable smartwatch platform without vendor lock-in.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AsteroidOS 2.0 发布，为开源智能手表操作系统带来新功能和改进，面向重视隐私的可穿戴设备用户。此更新显示社区仍在积极开发，提供了一个基于 Linux、可高度定制且无厂商锁定的替代选择。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://asteroidos.org/public/img/logo.png' data-full='https://asteroidos.org/public/img/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47051546' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/02/17/tesla-robotaxi-adds-5-more-crashes-austin-month-4x-worse-than-humans/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla &#x27;Robotaxi&#x27; adds 5 more crashes in Austin in a month – 4x worse than humans</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉“Robotaxi”在奥斯汀一个月内又添 5 起事故——比人类差 4 倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47051546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 455</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47051546' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 264</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reported cluster of crashes involving Tesla&#x27;s Robotaxi service in Austin added five incidents in a month, with claims the autonomous fleet is crashing at higher rates than human drivers. If accurate, the pattern raises questions about deployment readiness and the validity of claims that robotaxis are safer than human-operated vehicles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称特斯拉在奥斯汀的 Robotaxi 服务一个月内又发生五起事故，有报道称该自动车队的事故率高于人类司机。若属实，这一趋势将对自动驾驶投放成熟度及其比人类更安全的论断提出质疑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2021/08/Tesla-Full-Self-Driving-Beta-Hero.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2021/08/Tesla-Full-Self-Driving-Beta-Hero.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47052941' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(46) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Zaneham/BarraCUDA' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>BarraCUDA Open-source CUDA compiler targeting AMD GPUs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>BarraCUDA：面向 AMD GPU 的开源 CUDA 编译器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47052941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 450</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47052941' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 194</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>BarraCUDA is an open-source project that compiles CUDA code to run on AMD GPUs, aiming to bridge NVIDIA-centric CUDA ecosystems and AMD hardware. It could help developers reuse CUDA workloads on non-NVIDIA devices, lowering friction for heterogeneous GPU environments, though compatibility and performance trade-offs will determine adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>BarraCUDA 是个开源项目，目标是把 CUDA 代码编译到 AMD GPU 上，试图缩小以 NVIDIA 为中心的 CUDA 生态与 AMD 硬件之间的差距。它能让开发者在非 NVIDIA 设备上复用 CUDA 工作负载，但兼容性和性能权衡将决定其采用程度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1535270acea945dd40b2bf5d95a8cce2750674805a562806b85adc7e23be1f1a/Zaneham/BarraCUDA' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1535270acea945dd40b2bf5d95a8cce2750674805a562806b85adc7e23be1f1a/Zaneham/BarraCUDA' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47066241' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://old.reddit.com/r/MacOS/comments/1r8900z/macos_which_officially_supports_27_year_old/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>27-year-old Apple iBooks can connect to Wi-Fi and download official updates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>27 年前的 Apple iBooks 能连 Wi‑Fi 并下载官方更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47066241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 446</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47066241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 284</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An old Apple iBook from 1999 was able to connect to Wi‑Fi and receive official updates, demonstrating surprising longevity in legacy hardware and protocol compatibility. The find highlights how long-lived standards and minimal server-side compatibility can keep vintage devices functional in modern networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一台 1999 年的 Apple iBook 能够连接 Wi‑Fi 并下载官方更新，显示出旧硬件在协议兼容性方面的惊人持久性。这个发现强调了长期存在的标准和服务器端的向后兼容性如何让老设备在现代网络中继续可用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47095134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 444</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47095134' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 262</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一股草根隐私行动潮 intelligence 地点针对 Flock 的监控摄像头，凸显公民自由与数据收集的担忧。文章探讨社区做法、法律考量以及可能的政策回应。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(49) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asahilinux.org/2026/02/progress-report-6-19/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Asahi Linux Progress Report: Linux 6.19</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Asahi Linux 进展报告：Linux 6.19</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47059275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 437</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47059275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Asahi Linux team reports progress toward upstreaming support for Linux 6.19 on Apple Silicon, detailing driver work, performance fixes, and remaining hardware compatibility tasks. The update signals steady advancement in making mainline kernels usable on newer Mac hardware, reducing the gap for Linux users on Apple devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Asahi Linux 团队汇报了在 Apple Silicon 上支持 Linux 6.19 的进展，介绍了驱动开发、性能修复以及尚需解决的硬件兼容任务。该更新表明在使主线内核可用于新 Mac 硬件方面正在稳步推进，逐步缩小 Apple 设备上运行 Linux 的差距。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://asahilinux.org/img/AsahiLinux_logomark_256px.png' data-full='https://asahilinux.org/img/AsahiLinux_logomark_256px.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/us-plans-online-portal-bypass-content-bans-europe-elsewhere-2026-02-18/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US plans online portal to bypass content bans in Europe and elsewhere</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美方拟设在线门户以绕过欧洲等地的内容禁令</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47067270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 431</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47067270' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 814</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The plan envisions a digital channel to circumvent platform bans in Europe and other regions, raising questions about jurisdiction, censorship circumvention, and the potential for policy loopholes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这一计划设想建立在线通道以绕过欧洲及其他地区的内容禁令，引发关于管辖权、审查规避及潜在政策漏洞的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(51) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dolphin-emu.org/blog/2026/02/16/rise-of-the-triforce/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Rise of the Triforce</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三角力量的崛起</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47040524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 428</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47040524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>&quot;Rise of the Triforce&quot; is a progress update on Dolphin Emulator, detailing major achievements and future plans for enhancing GameCube and Wii game emulation. The post showcases technical milestones like improved compatibility and performance optimizations. It reflects the vibrant open-source community dedicated to preserving classic gaming experiences through advanced emulation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《三角力量的崛起》是Dolphin模拟器的最新进展报告，介绍了GameCube和Wii游戏模拟方面的重大成就与未来规划。文中展示了兼容性提升和性能优化等技术里程碑，体现了开放源代码社区通过先进模拟技术保护经典游戏的活力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://dolphin-emu.org/m/user/uploads/zinnia/2026/02/16/triforce-header-social.jpg' data-full='https://dolphin-emu.org/m/user/uploads/zinnia/2026/02/16/triforce-header-social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47088685' data-tags='Culture,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(52) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://harpers.org/archive/2026/03/childs-play-sam-kriss-ai-startup-roy-lee/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Child&#x27;s Play: Tech&#x27;s new generation and the end of thinking</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科技的儿童化：新一代与思考能力的终结</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47088685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 424</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47088685' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 258</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique of how tech and startup culture may encourage passive consumption and erode critical thinking. It argues for preserving human judgment in design and development to avoid over-reliance on automation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批评科技与初创文化可能让新一代陷入被动使用、削弱批判性思维。主张在设计与开发中保留人类判断，避免对自动化的过度依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wp.harpers.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/CUT-12-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://wp.harpers.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/CUT-12-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47049479' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(53) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://go.dev/blog/gofix' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Using go fix to modernize Go code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 go fix 现代化 Go 代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 424</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049479' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A guide explains how to use go fix to automatically apply idiomatic modernizations and migration changes to Go codebases, easing upgrades to newer language versions. It walks through common transforms and cautions on review and testing, highlighting go fix as a practical tool for large-scale refactors with minimal manual edits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇指南介绍如何使用 go fix 自动对 Go 代码库进行符合现代风格和版本迁移的改造，从而简化升级到新语言版本的工作。文中列举常见的转换并提醒要注意代码审查与测试，强调 go fix 是大规模重构时减少手工修改的实用工具。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gentoo.org/news/2026/02/16/codeberg.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gentoo on Codeberg</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gentoo 登陆 Codeberg</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47050067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 419</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47050067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gentoo announced hosting some of its repositories and infrastructure on Codeberg, signaling a move toward decentralized or alternative code hosting options. The shift reflects broader interest in diversifying hosting providers and aligning with communities favoring open-source-friendly, European-based platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gentoo 宣布将其部分仓库与基础设施迁至 Codeberg，表明其在寻求更分散或替代的代码托管选项。此举反映了在多样化托管服务商以及倾向于开源友好、欧洲平台的社区中日益增长的兴趣。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47042136' data-tags='Open Source,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/ai-is-destroying-open-source/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI is destroying open source, and it&#x27;s not even good yet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI正在摧毁开源，且还远不成熟</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47042136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 406</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47042136' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 332</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The rapid influx of AI-generated content is disrupting open source ecosystems, often flooding repositories with low-quality or redundant code. Despite AI’s potential, current implementations risk undermining community standards, maintainability, and collaboration culture. This warns that harnessing AI in open source requires careful curation and new governance models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI生成内容的快速涌入扰乱了开源生态，常导致大量低质量或重复代码充斥仓库。尽管AI具潜力，但现有应用风险削弱社区标准、维护性和协作氛围。提醒我们要在开源中利用AI，必须审慎管理和创新治理模式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.freecad.org/index.php' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FreeCAD</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47047010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 403</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47047010' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FreeCAD is an open-source, parametric 3D CAD tool with extensible architecture and scripting. It’s popular for hobbyists and engineers alike, offering customizable workflows for mechanical design and hobby projects without licensing fees.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FreeCAD 是一款开源的参数化 3D CAD 工具，具备可扩展的架构与脚本化能力。它在爱好者与工程师之间都很受欢迎，提供可自定义的工作流用于机械设计与原型开发，且无许可费。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47115805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 400</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47115805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 318</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reportedly, Google restricted Google AI Pro/Ultra subscribers from using OpenClaw, highlighting friction between premium tooling ecosystems and access controls. The incident underscores how platform policies can affect power users and third-party tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有用户声称 Google AI Pro/Ultra 用户在使用 OpenClaw 时遭遇账户限制，且未事先警告。事件凸显了高端工具生态与访问控制之间的矛盾，以及平台策略对开发者的影响。</p>
