<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece argues about the trade-offs of packaging Claude as an Electron app, balancing cross-platform ease and performance costs. It explains how app size, memory usage, and update cadence affect user experience and developer choices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文讨论将 Claude 作为 Electron 应用打包的取舍，权衡跨平台便利性与性能成本。应用体积、内存占用和更新节奏等因素如何影响用户体验与开发者选择。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chromereleases.googleblog.com/2026/02/stable-channel-update-for-desktop_13.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zero-day CSS: CVE-2026-2441 exists in the wild</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>零日 CSS：CVE-2026-2441 已在野外利用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47062748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 375</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47062748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 212</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A zero-day vulnerability identified as CVE-2026-2441 affects CSS processing and is reportedly being exploited in the wild, prompting urgent browser updates. The issue underscores that presentation-layer bugs can have real security impact and that prompt patching of browsers is critical for users and administrators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个编号为 CVE-2026-2441 的零日漏洞影响 CSS 解析，并被报道已在野外被利用，促使浏览器进行紧急更新。此事提醒我们表现层的缺陷也能带来实际安全风险，用户和管理员应及时打补丁。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEjP1xLDmFLSwhDBm3s-ybDpo-Wh_uM2bjqmbh5PiX-svixf-idIIDE-Nd6oLSHvvnpAql9hyPKBtK1zSufmg8RS1g16YmCZbbzPMhw72S7Dv7JBojpNzzMj5qDQtkAewk8K_r3mrRSGiPZs/s1600/googlelogo_color_200x200.png' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEjP1xLDmFLSwhDBm3s-ybDpo-Wh_uM2bjqmbh5PiX-svixf-idIIDE-Nd6oLSHvvnpAql9hyPKBtK1zSufmg8RS1g16YmCZbbzPMhw72S7Dv7JBojpNzzMj5qDQtkAewk8K_r3mrRSGiPZs/s1600/googlelogo_color_200x200.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47096253' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/karpathy/status/2024987174077432126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claws are now a new layer on top of LLM agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claws 成为大语言模型代理的新层</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47096253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 374</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47096253' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 839</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claws adds a new layer on top of LLM agents to improve safety, governance, and composability of autonomous tasks. It aims to provide a modular framework that handles orchestration, policy enforcement, and error handling beyond a single prompt. This could advance reliable agent AI, though adoption details remain to be seen.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claws 在大语言模型代理之上引入新的一层，提升自治任务的安全性、治理与模块化能力，提供用于编排、策略执行和错误处理的分层框架。此举有望推动更可靠的代理式 AI，但具体落地与普及情况尚待观察。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1296667294148382721/9Pr6XrPB_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1296667294148382721/9Pr6XrPB_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47035679' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rockpapershotgun.com/good-news-uk-discord-users-were-part-of-a-peter-thiel-linked-data-collection-experiment' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>UK Discord users were part of a Peter Thiel-linked data collection experiment</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国Discord用户参与了彼得·蒂尔相关数据采集实验</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47035679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 374</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47035679' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>UK Discord users were unknowingly involved in a data collection experiment linked to investor Peter Thiel, raising privacy and ethical concerns. This revelation spotlights the growing use of popular social platforms for covert research or data harvesting. It calls attention to the need for clearer consent and transparency in digital data practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国Discord用户在不知情的情况下参与了与投资人彼得·蒂尔相关的数据采集实验，引发隐私和伦理争议。这一揭露凸显了流行社交平台被用于隐秘研究或数据收集的趋势，强调数字数据操作中需加强明确同意和透明度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assetsio.gnwcdn.com/Discordageverification2.png?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;fit=crop&amp;enable=upscale&amp;auto=webp' data-full='https://assetsio.gnwcdn.com/Discordageverification2.png?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;fit=crop&amp;enable=upscale&amp;auto=webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47034192' data-tags='Hardware,AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mashable.com/article/ai-hard-drive-hdd-shortages-western-digital-sold-out' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Thanks a lot, AI: Hard drives are sold out for the year, says WD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>感谢AI：西部数据称硬盘全年售罄</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47034192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 373</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47034192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 306</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Western Digital announced that hard drives are sold out for the year, largely due to increased demand driven by AI workloads and data centers. This shortage reflects broader supply-demand imbalances as AI infrastructure scales rapidly. It highlights how AI growth can stress traditional hardware supply chains in unexpected ways.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>西部数据表示硬盘全年已售罄，主要因AI工作负载和数据中心需求激增。此短缺反映出随着AI基础设施快速扩展，供需失衡加剧，显示AI发展对传统硬件供应链带来意想不到的压力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://helios-i.mashable.com/imagery/articles/03BMp5tylVs9DJJavYCVFKV/hero-image.fill.size_1200x675.v1771180235.jpg' data-full='https://helios-i.mashable.com/imagery/articles/03BMp5tylVs9DJJavYCVFKV/hero-image.fill.size_1200x675.v1771180235.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devblogs.microsoft.com/azure-sql/langchain-with-sqlvectorstore-example/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft guide to pirating Harry Potter for LLM training (2024) [removed]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软关于为 LLM 训练盗版《哈利·波特》的指南（2024）[已移除]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47067759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 358</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47067759' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An apparently removed 2024 developer blog contained an example that could be read as demonstrating how copyrighted text was used for language-model workflows, raising questions about training data licensing. The snippet&#x27;s removal highlights ongoing tensions around permissive examples, copyright law, and responsible documentation for model training practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇已被移除的 2024 年开发博客中包含的示例，可能被解读为展示了如何将受版权保护文本用于语言模型流程，从而引发有关训练数据许可的问题。该内容被删凸显了示例文档、版权法与模型训练责任之间的持续紧张关系。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47040430' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.12670' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SkillsBench: Benchmarking how well agent skills work across diverse tasks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SkillsBench：跨多样任务评测代理技能表现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47040430' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 355</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47040430' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SkillsBench introduces a benchmark framework to systematically evaluate AI agent skills across a diverse range of tasks. It aims to better understand how different capabilities generalize beyond specialized scenarios, providing insights into agent robustness and versatility. This work supports advancing more reliable and adaptable AI agents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SkillsBench提出了一套评测框架，用于系统性评估AI代理在多样化任务中的技能表现。旨在了解各种能力在专门场景外的泛化效果，为代理的鲁棒性和多功能性提供洞察，助推更可靠适应性强的AI代理发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cleantechnica.com/2026/02/15/tesla-sales-down-tremendously-in-uk-norway-netherlands-germany-spain-sweden-denmark-portugal-switzerland/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla Sales Down 55% UK, 58% Spain, 59% Germany, 81% Netherlands, 93% Norway</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉销量大跌：英国降 55%，西班牙 58%，德国 59%，荷兰 81%，挪威 93%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47048052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 337</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47048052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 349</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reported regional sales figures show steep year-over-year declines for Tesla in multiple European markets, with drops as high as 93% in Norway. These numbers suggest local market dynamics, increased competition, shifting incentives, or production/supply issues are significantly impacting Tesla&#x27;s European sales performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报告显示特斯拉在多个欧洲市场的销量出现大幅同比下降，挪威降幅高达 93%。这些数字表明当地市场动态、竞争加剧、激励变化或产供问题正在显著影响特斯拉在欧洲的销售表现。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cleantechnica.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/Tesla-Model-S-Black-Low-Speed-Netherlands-Belgium-Europe-Zach-Shahan-CleanTechnica-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://cleantechnica.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/Tesla-Model-S-Black-Low-Speed-Netherlands-Belgium-Europe-Zach-Shahan-CleanTechnica-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/xaskasdf/ntransformer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Llama 3.1 70B on a single RTX 3090 via NVMe-to-GPU bypassing the CPU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：在单张 RTX 3090 上用 NVMe–GPU 直连实现 Llama 3.1 70B，绕过 CPU</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47104667' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 337</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47104667' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN demonstrates Llama 3.1 70B running on a single RTX 3090 by bypassing CPU bottlenecks with an NVMe-to-GPU path. The approach leans on streaming weights and memory-efficient techniques to fit a large model on consumer hardware, signaling a path for hobbyists and small teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了在单张 RTX 3090 上通过 NVMe–GPU 直连绕过 CPU 瓶颈来运行 Llama 3.1 70B。该方法依赖权重流式加载与节省内存的技巧，使大型模型在消费级硬件上可用，给爱好者和小团队开辟了新路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f0d4fb95d21a897c25a1b752b21861cb294e483389f056f30bed5ad9a5268dd2/xaskasdf/ntransformer' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f0d4fb95d21a897c25a1b752b21861cb294e483389f056f30bed5ad9a5268dd2/xaskasdf/ntransformer' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tadaima.bearblog.dev/privilege-is-bad-grammar/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Privilege is bad grammar</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特权是一种糟糕的语法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47038125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 330</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47038125' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 278</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author argues that &#x27;privilege&#x27; often gets misused in discourse, leading to misunderstandings and unproductive conversations. They suggest reframing privilege in clearer, more precise language to improve communication and foster constructive dialogue. This perspective encourages more thoughtful discussions on social dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为“特权”一词在讨论中常被滥用，引发误解和无效交流。建议用更清晰、精准的语言重新表达“特权”，以提升沟通效果，促进建设性对话。该观点推动更为深思熟虑的社会动态讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/herman-1683556668-0.png' data-full='https://bear-images.sfo2.cdn.digitaloceanspaces.com/herman-1683556668-0.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47064047' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://letsencrypt.org/2026/02/18/dns-persist-01.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DNS-Persist-01: A New Model for DNS-Based Challenge Validation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DNS-Persist-01：一种新的基于 DNS 的挑战验证模式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47064047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 315</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47064047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 155</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt introduced DNS-Persist-01, a new ACME challenge type that allows DNS-based validation to persist beyond short-lived records, simplifying automation for some use cases. This change can reduce flakiness in DNS validation workflows and help environments where DNS propagation or record stability is a problem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Let&#x27;s Encrypt 推出 DNS-Persist-01，一种新的 ACME 挑战类型，使基于 DNS 的验证能在短期记录之外保持，从而简化某些自动化场景。此改动可减少 DNS 验证流程中的不稳定性，利于 DNS 传播或记录稳定性较差的环境。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://letsencrypt.org/images/LetsEncrypt-SocialShare.png' data-full='https://letsencrypt.org/images/LetsEncrypt-SocialShare.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47082496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 312</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47082496' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 140</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers flag a popular Android emulator that runs a string of stealth reconnaissance commands on a fixed interval, suggesting possible spyware or misconfiguration; practitioners should audit emulators in app testing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全研究者发现流行的 Android 模拟器每 30 分钟悄无声息执行多条侦察命令，疑似间谍软件或配置问题。开发者在应用测试中应审计模拟器的行为。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.adafruit.com/2026/02/19/californias-new-bill-requires-doj-approved-3d-printers-that-report-on-themselves/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>California&#x27;s new bill requires DOJ-approved 3D printers that report themselves</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47077844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 312</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47077844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 367</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Legislation would require 3D printers to be DOJ-approved and to self-report usage data, raising questions about compliance, safety, and maker community impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法案规定 3D 打印机需经 DOJ 批准并自报使用数据，涉及合规性、安全性及对制造者社区的影响等问题。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47113618' data-tags='Open Source,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://joinloops.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Loops is a federated, open-source TikTok</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Loops：一个联邦化的开源 TikTok</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 312</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Loops is a federated, open-source TikTok-like platform aiming to decentralize social video. It uses federation to connect independent servers, potentially improving user control and moderation. The project signals a shift toward interoperable social apps beyond centralized goliaths.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Loops 是一个联邦化的开源短视频平台，目标是去中心化社交视频。通过服务器自治与互联，提升用户对数据与治理的控制，并探索可互操作的社交网格。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gamedate.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gamedate – A site to revive dead multiplayer games</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gamedate：一个让已停运的多人游戏重获新生的平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47096167' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 307</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47096167' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gamedate provides a venue for reviving abandoned multiplayer games by coordinating communities, matchmaking, and mods around legacy titles. It highlights community-driven preservation as a way to extend the life of classic games and reduce waste.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gamedate 为复兴已停运的多人游戏提供平台，帮助社区组织、重建匹配与模组，让经典作品重新活跃。此举体现通过社区力量延长游戏生命、降低资源浪费的趋势。</p>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.governance.fyi/p/america-vs-singapore-you-cant-save' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>America vs. Singapore: You can&#x27;t save your way out of economic shocks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国对比新加坡：你不能靠储蓄摆脱经济冲击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47074389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 305</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47074389' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 461</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comparative analysis argues that savings alone can&#x27;t shield economies from shocks; policy mix, diversification, and resilience investments matter.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对比分析认为，仅靠储蓄无法完全抵御经济冲击；需要多元化政策、韧性投资与结构性改革。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/paypal-discloses-data-breach-exposing-users-personal-information/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PayPal discloses data breach that exposed user info for 6 months</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PayPal 披露数据泄露：用户信息暴露长达 6 个月</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47087719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47087719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 88</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PayPal disclosed a data breach exposing user information for six months, prompting guidance for affected users to monitor accounts and enable alerts. The incident underscores fintech security challenges and the importance of timely incident response.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PayPal 公开披露数据泄露，用户信息暴露长达六个月，敦促受影响用户监控账户并开启通知。事件凸显金融科技领域的安全挑战及快速响应的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/02/20/PayPal-headpic.jpg' data-full='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2026/02/20/PayPal-headpic.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47038588' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://notnotp.com/notes/use-protocols-not-services/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Use protocols, not services</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用协议，而非服务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47038588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47038588' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay advocates for designing systems around open, standardized protocols instead of relying on proprietary services. Emphasizing protocols encourages interoperability, decentralization, and user control, aligning with foundational internet principles. This argument supports building resilient, user-empowering technology ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张围绕开放标准协议设计系统，而非依赖专有服务。强调协议促进互操作性、去中心化和用户控制，契合互联网基础理念。该观点支持构建更具弹性和用户自主权的技术生态。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://juno-labs.com/blogs/every-company-building-your-ai-assistant-is-an-ad-company' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every company building your AI assistant is now an ad company</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>打造你 AI 助手的每家公司现在都是广告公司</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47092203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 301</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47092203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 163</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that AI assistant developers monetize primarily through data-driven advertising and targeted services, raising privacy concerns. Calls for greater transparency, user controls, and consented data usage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出开发 AI 助手的公司多通过数据驱动的广告及定向服务来变现，带来隐私风险。呼吁提高透明度、提供用户控制与明确的同意数据使用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/LadybirdBrowser/ladybird/issues/933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Closing this as we are no longer pursuing Swift adoption</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project issue announcing the decision to stop pursuing Swift adoption cites practical reasons—likely maintenance burden, ecosystem fit, or contributor availability—leading to reverting course. It highlights the trade-offs teams face when evaluating language migrations and the importance of aligning tooling choices with project capacity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一条议题声明决定不再推进 Swift 的采纳，理由可能是维护负担、生态适配或贡献者可用性等实际因素，因而选择回退。此事凸显团队在评估语言迁移时的权衡，以及将工具选择与项目能力对齐的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47086980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 296</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47086980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 329</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The larger deal was abandoned in favor of a smaller investment, reshaping partnerships and governance in the AI ecosystem. The move signals recalibrated expectations for collaboration and access to compute power.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>原本的高额交易被放弃，改为较小规模的投资，重新塑造了 AI 生态中的伙伴关系与治理结构，折射出对合作与算力获取的再评估。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Feb/21/claws/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Andrej Karpathy talks about &quot;Claws&quot;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Andrej Karpathy 讨论“Claws”</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47099160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 281</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47099160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Karpathy explains Claws, framing it as a middleware layer that manages agent behavior and tool use, potentially enabling more controllable, multi-agent AI systems. It suggests a path toward composable AI where planners and executors are decoupled.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Karpathy 将 Claws 解释为管理代理行为与工具使用的中间层，或将实现更易控、可扩展的多代理 AI 系统。暗示了将规划与执行解耦、实现可组合 AI 的发展路径。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47068948' data-tags='Gaming,Hardware,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gamingonlinux.com/2026/02/minecraft-java-is-switching-from-opengl-to-vulkan-for-the-vibrant-visuals-update/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Minecraft Java is switching from OpenGL to Vulkan</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Minecraft Java 将从 OpenGL 转向 Vulkan</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47068948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 278</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47068948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 171</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Minecraft Java is switching its rendering backend from OpenGL to Vulkan as part of a visual upgrade, promising better performance and more modern GPU features. The move reflects broader industry trends toward explicit, lower-level graphics APIs that offer improved multi-threading and driver predictability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Minecraft Java 将其渲染后端从 OpenGL 改为 Vulkan，作为视觉升级的一部分，承诺带来更好的性能和现代 GPU 功能。此举符合业界朝向更底层、更明确的图形 API 的趋势，这类 API 在多线程和驱动可预测性方面更有优势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gamingonlinux.com/uploads/articles/tagline_images/1519041173id28520gol.jpg' data-full='https://www.gamingonlinux.com/uploads/articles/tagline_images/1519041173id28520gol.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47049088' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/2026/02/16/semantic_ablation_ai_writing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Semantic ablation: Why AI writing is generic and boring</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>语义消融：为何 AI 写作平淡乏味</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049088' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049088' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 214</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that AI-generated writing often feels generic because models lose high-level semantic structure during generation, producing plausible but uninteresting prose. It points to training, objective functions, and evaluation mismatches as root causes and suggests techniques for restoring distinct voice and purposeful variation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 AI 生成的文本常显得千篇一律，是因为模型在生成过程中丧失了高层语义结构，产出可行但缺乏趣味的文段。文中把问题归结于训练目标与评估不匹配，并提出一些恢复独特风格和有意变异的技术方向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://regmedia.co.uk/2021/04/23/dog.jpg' data-full='https://regmedia.co.uk/2021/04/23/dog.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47055696' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://status.pki.goog/incidents/5oJEbcU3ZfMfySTSXXd3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Public CA is down</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google 公共证书机构宕机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47055696' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 276</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47055696' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google&#x27;s Public CA experienced an outage, causing issues for TLS certificate issuance or validation in some services. Such CA incidents can have broad knock-on effects for automated provisioning, client trust chains, and services that rely on fresh certificates or CRL/OCSP responses.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 的公共证书机构发生故障，导致某些服务在 TLS 证书签发或验证方面出现问题。证书机构的故障可能对自动化配置、客户端信任链以及依赖最新证书或 CRL/OCSP 响应的服务产生广泛连锁影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://status.pki.goog/images/trust-logo.svg' data-full='https://status.pki.goog/images/trust-logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47062521' data-tags='Business,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://elliotbonneville.com/the-only-moat-left-is-money/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The only moat left is money?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>剩下的唯一护城河是钱？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47062521' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 276</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47062521' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 382</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay argues that in some tech markets, capital—ability to burn cash and outspend rivals—has become the primary moat, rather than technological advantage or product differentiation. It examines implications for competition, innovation incentives, and market structure when money, not sustainable defensibility, decides winners.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出在某些科技市场中，资本——即烧钱并压倒对手的能力——已经成为主要护城河，而非技术优势或产品差异化。文中探讨了当金钱而非可持续的防御能力决定胜负时，对竞争、创新激励和市场结构的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47049718' data-tags='Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://practical.engineering/blog/2026/2/17/so-you-want-to-build-a-tunnel' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>So you want to build a tunnel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>所以你想建一条隧道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 271</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An engineering primer explains the technical, geological, and logistical challenges of designing and constructing tunnels, from alignment and boring methods to ventilation and emergency systems. It emphasizes cost drivers, risk management, and how long timelines and regulatory hurdles shape major tunneling projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇工程入门文介绍了设计和建造隧道的技术、地质与后勤难点，包括线路选择、掘进方法以及通风与应急系统。文章强调了成本驱动因素、风险管理，以及漫长工期和监管障碍如何影响大型隧道工程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/55ac34e4e4b0170ef54be632/55ac3907e4b03d1c8c9deff1/69949856d3302d5668e7e2d6/1771346099695/Hobby-Tunnels_1a+%281%29.jpg?format=1500w' data-full='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/55ac34e4e4b0170ef54be632/55ac3907e4b03d1c8c9deff1/69949856d3302d5668e7e2d6/1771346099695/Hobby-Tunnels_1a+%281%29.jpg?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/2026/02/19/irs_job_cuts/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IRS lost 40% of IT staff, 80% of tech leaders in &#x27;efficiency&#x27; shakeup</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IRS 在‘效率’改革中流失大量 IT 人才</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47077849' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 270</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47077849' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The IRS overhaul aimed at efficiency has gutted IT leadership, potentially slowing modernization efforts and increasing risk for critical taxpayer systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>为追求效率进行的改革让 IRS 的 IT 领导层大量流失，可能放慢现代化进程并提高关键系统风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://regmedia.co.uk/2025/03/03/shutterstock_job_loss.jpg' data-full='https://regmedia.co.uk/2025/03/03/shutterstock_job_loss.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/microsoft/microsoft-says-bug-causes-copilot-to-summarize-confidential-emails/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft says bug causes Copilot to summarize confidential emails</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软称漏洞导致 Copilot 摘要机密邮件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47060202' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 268</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47060202' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft acknowledged a bug in Copilot that could summarize confidential emails, potentially exposing sensitive content via assistant features. This incident highlights privacy risks of AI assistants integrated into corporate mailflows and the need for strict context-awareness and access controls in such tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软承认 Copilot 存在一个漏洞，可能将机密邮件内容生成摘要，从而有泄露敏感信息的风险。此事凸显了将 AI 助手整合入企业邮箱流时的隐私风险，强调需要在这些工具中实施严格的上下文感知和访问控制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2025/09/16/Copilot_headpic.jpg' data-full='https://www.bleepstatic.com/content/hl-images/2025/09/16/Copilot_headpic.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47048004' data-tags='Data,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(95) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aeris.edbn.me/?city=SFO' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I converted 2D conventional flight tracking into 3D</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我把二维常规航班追踪转成了三维</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47048004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 267</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47048004' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author converted conventional 2D flight-tracking data into 3D visualizations for airports like SFO, enriching situational awareness with altitude and trajectory insights. The 3D view helps spot vertical patterns, approach paths, and congestions that 2D overlays can obscure, useful for enthusiasts and operational analysis alike.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者将传统的二维航班追踪数据转换为三维可视化（如 SFO），通过高度与轨迹展现增强了态势感知。三维视图有助于识别垂直模式、进场航线与拥堵情况，这些在二维叠加图中往往不易看清，对爱好者和运营分析都有价值。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47098582' data-tags='AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(96) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/alvi-se/ai-ublock-blacklist' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI uBlock Blacklist</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 版 uBlock 黑名单</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47098582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 267</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47098582' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This repository curates ad-blocking filters leveraging AI to identify and block content that resembles ads or trackers, aiming to improve the effectiveness of ad blockers in AI-enabled browsing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该仓库提供用于 uBlock Origin 等广告拦截器的 AI 驱动过滤清单，旨在更准确地识别并屏蔽与 AI 相关的广告和跟踪。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/zachlatta/freeflow' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Free alternative to Wispr Flow, Superwhisper, and Monologue</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>展示 HN：Wispr Flow、Superwhisper 和 Monologue 的免费替代品</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47040375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 267</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47040375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new open-source project offers a free alternative to popular communication tools like Wispr Flow, Superwhisper, and Monologue. It aims to provide comparable functionality without subscription fees, appealing to privacy-conscious users and developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款新的开源项目提供了Wispr Flow、Superwhisper和Monologue等流行通信工具的免费替代品。它致力于在无订阅费用的情况下，向注重隐私的用户和开发者提供类似功能。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47050376' data-tags='Web,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(98) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kotaku.com/discord-alternative-teamspeak-age-verification-check-rivals-2000669693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Discord Rival Gets Overwhelmed by Exodus of Players Fleeing Age-Verification</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Discord 竞争者因年龄验证引发玩家大量流失而不堪重负</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款 Discord 的替代平台在实行严格年龄验证后遭遇用户大规模流失，导致平台被突增的账户问题和审核负担压垮。该事件展示了合规要求与用户体验之间的权衡，以及摩擦如何侵蚀社区平台的网络效应。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.koreaherald.com/article/10679206' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>CXMT has been offering DDR4 chips at about half the prevailing market rate</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>CXMT 正以约市场价一半的价格提供 DDR4 芯片</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47101171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 250</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47101171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 251</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>CXMT has been offering DDR4 chips at about half the prevailing market rate. This aggressive pricing hints at oversupply, regional subsidies, or strategic dumping that could pressure rivals. Buyers may benefit from lower costs but should watch for quality variance, supply stability, and downstream pricing impacts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，CXMT 以约市场价一半的价格出售 DDR4 芯片。这表明可能存在产能过剩、政府补贴或策略性抛售等因素，可能压低行业价格。买家应关注质量、供货稳定性，以及对下游厂商和市场格局的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gustedt.wordpress.com/2026/02/15/defer-available-in-gcc-and-clang/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Defer available in gcc and clang</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GCC 与 Clang 实现 defer 机制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47031197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 240</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47031197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 217</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A macro-based pattern enables defer-like cleanup semantics in GCC and Clang, letting code register actions to run at scope exit. This brings Go-style resource management to C/C++ without changing language standards, easing RAII-style cleanup across blocks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GCC 与 Clang 通过一个宏模式实现类似 Go 的 defer 语义，使代码能够在作用域退出时执行清理操作。为 C/C++ 引入一种无需修改语言标准的资源管理方式，提升跨块的清理一致性，但需注意对控制流的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gustedt.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/raven.png' data-full='https://gustedt.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/raven.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(109) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.harudagondi.space/blog/parse-dont-validate-and-type-driven-design-in-rust/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Parse, Don&#x27;t Validate and Type-Driven Design in Rust</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 的“解析优先”与类型驱动设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47103931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 235</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47103931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Promotes parsing-first validation and type-driven design in Rust, arguing that the right types should encode invariants and that parsing should produce clean, well-formed data. The approach leverages Rust’s strong type system—newtypes, enums, and type-level guarantees—to reduce runtime validation and make API boundaries safer.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提倡在 Rust 中采用解析优先的验证和类型驱动设计，即通过类型对不变量进行编码，让解析直接产出正确的数据结构。依托强类型系统（新类型、枚举、类型级保证）来减少运行时校验，使 API 边界更安全。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.harudagondi.space/uploads/parse-dont-validate/tingey-injury-law-firm-veNb0DDegzE-unsplash.jpg' data-full='https://www.harudagondi.space/uploads/parse-dont-validate/tingey-injury-law-firm-veNb0DDegzE-unsplash.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47065678' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(113) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://science.nasa.gov/climate-change/evidence/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There is unequivocal evidence that Earth is warming (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有确凿证据表明地球在变暖（2024）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47065678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 228</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47065678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 280</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A summary presents unequivocal evidence that Earth is warming, compiling temperature records, ice loss, sea-level rise, and ecological impacts as of 2024. The collection reinforces the scientific consensus and underlines the urgency for mitigation and adaptation policies informed by these observed trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份汇总展示了确凿证据表明地球正在变暖，集合了气温记录、冰层消融、海平面上升与生态影响等至 2024 年的数据。该资料强化了科学共识，并强调基于这些观测趋势采取缓解与适应政策的紧迫性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.science.nasa.gov/content/dam/science/esd/climate/internal_resources/2715/evidence_banner_new_1920.jpeg/jcr:content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg' data-full='https://assets.science.nasa.gov/content/dam/science/esd/climate/internal_resources/2715/evidence_banner_new_1920.jpeg/jcr:content/renditions/cq5dam.web.1280.1280.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47111400' data-tags='Security,Hardware,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.popsci.com/technology/robot-vacuum-army/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Man accidentally gains control of 7k robot vacuums</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>男子意外获得对约 7000 台扫地机器人的控制权</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47111400' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47111400' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A man inadvertently gains control of around 7,000 robotic vacuums, exposing how insecure IoT deployments can be weaponized or misused. The incident spotlights the need for safer defaults, firmware updates, and robust access controls in consumer robotics. It also raises questions about surveillance perimeter and botnet risk in dense deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名男子在不经意间获得对约 7000 台扫地机器人的控制权，暴露了物联设备的安全漏洞。此事件凸显了对普通消费级机器人设定更安全的默认设置、固件更新与访问控制的必要性。与此同时也引发对在密集部署中的监控与僵尸网络风险的关注。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.popsci.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/robot_army.jpg?quality=85&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://www.popsci.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/robot_army.jpg?quality=85&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://environment.ec.europa.eu/news/new-law-more-sustainable-circular-and-safe-batteries-enters-force-2023-08-17_en' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EU mandates replaceable batteries by 2027 (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧盟要求到 2027 年实现可更换电池（2023 年规定）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47098687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 226</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47098687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 176</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU rules mandate replaceable batteries to boost repairability and sustainability. The policy impacts product design, battery standards, and the circular economy, potentially increasing upfront costs but extending device lifespans.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟新规要求产品具备可更换电池以提升可修复性与可持续性，影响产品设计、电池标准与循环经济，可能提高初期成本但延长设备使用寿命。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://environment.ec.europa.eu/sites/default/files/styles/ewcms_metatag_image/public/2023-08/Sustainable%20batteries%20image.jpeg?itok=_zHg03-f' data-full='https://environment.ec.europa.eu/sites/default/files/styles/ewcms_metatag_image/public/2023-08/Sustainable%20batteries%20image.jpeg?itok=_zHg03-f' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47091606' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(116) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.neuroai.science/p/blue-light-filters-dont-work' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Blue light filters don&#x27;t work – controlling total luminance is a better bet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蓝光滤镜不起作用——总亮度控制才是关键</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47091606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 225</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47091606' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 231</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Evidence suggests blue-light filters offer limited benefits for eye strain or sleep. System-wide luminance controls and adaptive brightness are more effective in reducing visual discomfort and circadian disruption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明蓝光滤镜对缓解眼疲劳或改善睡眠的效果有限，系统级的总亮度控制与自适应亮度更能有效降低视觉不适与昼夜节律干扰。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!ZstF!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F0d43665d-dcdb-4bce-a926-fe8bb91fa185_680x364.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!ZstF!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F0d43665d-dcdb-4bce-a926-fe8bb91fa185_680x364.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenScan 开放扫描项目</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47049628' data-tags='Programming,AI,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(118) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.vectorware.com/blog/async-await-on-gpu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Async/Await on the GPU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPU 上的 Async/Await</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 224</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 54</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post explores implementing async/await-style concurrency on GPUs, tackling challenges like scheduling, memory models, and latency hiding in massively parallel hardware. If practical, these ideas could simplify GPU programming by expressing asynchronous workflows more naturally, but they require runtime and hardware support to be efficient.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨在 GPU 上实现类似 async/await 的并发模型，涉及在大规模并行硬件中调度、内存模型与隐藏延迟等挑战。若可行，这些想法能通过更自然地表达异步工作流来简化 GPU 编程，但要高效运行仍需运行时和硬件层面的支持。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.vectorware.com/_next/image/?url=%2F_next%2Fstatic%2Fmedia%2Fvectorware_logo.6d5f5210.png&amp;w=640&amp;q=75&amp;dpl=dpl_AM1C5nwhzYXTUSarDfD6tMzFtB8g' data-full='https://www.vectorware.com/_next/image/?url=%2F_next%2Fstatic%2Fmedia%2Fvectorware_logo.6d5f5210.png&amp;w=640&amp;q=75&amp;dpl=dpl_AM1C5nwhzYXTUSarDfD6tMzFtB8g' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47111067' data-tags='Politics,Privacy,Security,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/c5yj2kzkrj0o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Iran students stage first large anti-government protests since deadly crackdown</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>伊朗学生在致命镇压后首次举行大规模抗议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47111067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 224</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47111067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Iranian students have staged sizable anti-government demonstrations, marking the first major protests since a deadly crackdown. The movement underscores rising student activism and the delicate balance of security measures and civil dissent. The tech angle includes monitoring, internet access controls, and how digital tools shape protest organization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>伊朗学生在致命镇压后首次举行大规模抗议，标志着抗议热潮的延续。这一行动凸显了学生群体在政治变局中的积极作用，以及政府维稳的挑战。数字工具在组织与监控方面的作用也成为关注点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47110473' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://planetscale.com/blog/database-transactions' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is a database transaction?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数据库事务是什么</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47110473' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 220</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47110473' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A database transaction is a sequence of operations treated as a single unit, guaranteeing atomicity, consistency, isolation, and durability (ACID). It matters for data integrity under concurrent workloads and in error handling, with practical examples like transfers or multi-step updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数据库事务是一组作为一个原子单元执行的操作，确保原子性、一致性、隔离性和持久性（ACID）。在并发工作负载和错误处理场景中，它对数据完整性至关重要，通过诸如转账或多步更新等示例直观展示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://planetscale.com/assets/database-transactions-social-C4_q_qA5.png' data-full='https://planetscale.com/assets/database-transactions-social-C4_q_qA5.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇研究式博客展示了在约 40MB 的二进制文件中有意隐藏后门的情形，并用 AI 与 Ghidra 来检测它们。说明了自动化分析在大规模二进制检测中的作用及潜在威胁。给红队演练与蓝队防御提供了实用经验教训。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://martinfowler.com/fragments/2026-02-18.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Future of AI Software Development</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 软件开发的未来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47062534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 201</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47062534' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A forward-looking piece discusses how AI will reshape software development workflows, from coding assistants and automated testing to higher-level design synthesis. It argues that teams must redesign processes, tooling, and review practices to capture productivity benefits while managing risks like hallucinations and degraded code quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇前瞻性文章讨论了 AI 将如何重塑软件开发流程，涵盖从代码助手与自动化测试到更高层的设计生成。作者认为团队需要重构流程、工具和评审机制，以在管理幻觉和代码质量风险的同时，真正获取生产力提升。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Critiques a culture that equates taste with credentialed skill, arguing for more inclusive standards in tech design and hiring. It champions craft, experimentation, and user-centered approaches over elitist credentials.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>批评将品味等同于资历的文化，主张在科技设计与招聘中采用更包容的标准，强调工艺、试验性与以用户为中心的做法，而非对资历的排他性依赖。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eduke32.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EDuke32 – Duke Nukem 3D (Open-Source)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EDuke32：Duke Nukem 3D 的开源实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47104185' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47104185' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EDuke32 is a modern open-source port of Duke Nukem 3D built on the Build engine, bringing updated rendering and cross-platform support to a classic shooter. It adds tooling for mods and expansions, inviting preservation-minded players and hobby developers to experiment with source code and assets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EDuke32 是 Duke Nukem 3D 的开源移植，基于 Build 引擎，提供现代渲染和跨平台支持。它为模组和扩展提供工具，适合热爱复古游戏的玩家与开发者探索源码与资产。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AGENTS.md旨在规范编码代理的提示设计，但其提升代理行为的效果仍在评估中。了解其优缺点对于推动自主编码助手的发展至关重要。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techdirt.com/2026/02/19/doge-bros-grant-review-process-was-literally-just-asking-chatgpt-is-this-dei/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DOGE Bro&#x27;s Grant Review Process Was Literally Just Asking ChatGPT &#x27;Is This DEI?&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DOGE Bros 的资助评审流程其实就是在请 ChatGPT 问 &#x27;这是不是 DEI？&#x27;</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47076826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 187</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47076826' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A lighthearted or investigative piece tracks recurring UFO sightings and the pattern of anticipation around them, blending skepticism and cultural commentary. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇带有调侃或调查意味的文章追踪反复出现的不明飞行物目击事件及围绕它们的期待情绪，兼具怀疑与文化评论。文章反思了对非凡事件的渴望、媒体循环与社交动力如何在证据不足时仍推动不断的主张传播。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.governance.fyi/p/americas-pensions-cant-beat-a-vanguard' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>America&#x27;s pensions can&#x27;t beat Vanguard but they can close a hospital</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国养老基金打不过先锋，但能关掉一家医院</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47048248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 183</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47048248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 341</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis shows public pension funds struggle to match low-cost index returns like Vanguard&#x27;s, and in trying to outperform they sometimes make local investment choices that impact communities—such as hospital closures. The piece explores the governance and political pressures that shape pension allocations, often with unintended social consequences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇分析指出，美国公共养老基金难以匹敌像先锋这种低成本指数的回报，且为追求超额收益会做出影响本地社区的投资决策，例如导致医院关闭。文章探讨了影响养老基金配置的治理与政治压力，这些决策常带来意料之外的社会后果。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mental exhaustion burns negligible calories but can significantly impair physical workout performance afterward. This finding highlights the complex relationship between cognitive load and athletic output.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A16z partner argues that the idea we’ll vibe code everything is wrong; real-world product success requires human insight, domain context, and governance. The piece urges founders to pair automation with judgment and ethics.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47105198' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/doi/10.1126/science.adq7188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Evidence of the bouba-kiki effect in naïve baby chicks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>未经训练的小鸡对 Bouba–Kiki 效应的证据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47105198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47105198' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The bouba–kiki effect appears in naïve chicks, suggesting early cross-modal mappings are not unique to humans. The finding informs debates on perceptual biases, cognition, and language evolution across species.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在未经训练的小鸡中也观察到 Bouba–Kiki 效应，表明早期感知-形象映射并非人类独有。此发现为跨物种的感知偏好、认知与语言起源研究提供证据与启示。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new project offers a lightweight analytics/metabase alternative built on DuckDB, aiming for fast, local analytic queries with a simpler stack. It targets teams that want easy setup, embedded SQL analytics, and lower operational overhead compared to heavier BI platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个新项目基于 DuckDB 提供轻量级的分析/Metabase 替代方案，主打快速的本地分析查询和更简洁的技术栈。该项目面向那些希望比重量级 BI 平台更易部署、支持内嵌 SQL 分析且运维开销更低的团队。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://adamj.eu/tech/2023/09/18/git-dont-create-gitkeep/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Don&#x27;t create .gitkeep files, use .gitignore instead (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不要创建 .gitkeep 文件，改用 .gitignore</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47094877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47094877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues for using .gitignore to ignore empty directories rather than keeping .gitkeep placeholders. It covers practical patterns and pitfalls, helping maintain a clean repo.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张用 .gitignore 忽略空目录，而不是添加 .gitkeep 作为占位。文章给出实际的模式和注意事项，帮助保持代码仓库整洁。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47046070' data-tags='Programming,Data,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://floedb.ai/blog/two-bits-are-better-than-one-making-bloom-filters-2x-more-accurate' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Two Bits Are Better Than One: making bloom filters 2x more accurate</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>两个比特比一个更好：让布隆过滤器精确度提升两倍</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47046070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47046070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two bits per Bloom filter cell can halve false positives compared to the traditional single-bit version. The approach trades a modest memory increase for much higher accuracy, benefiting large caches, search systems, and network infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>布隆过滤器的每个单元用两个比特即可将误报率降至原来的一半左右。该做法在增加少量内存开销的同时显著提升准确性，适用于大规模缓存、搜索系统和网络基础设施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://floedb.ai/hubfs/BlogCover/bloom_filter.png' data-full='https://floedb.ai/hubfs/BlogCover/bloom_filter.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47068320' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cepr.org/voxeu/columns/how-ai-affecting-productivity-and-jobs-europe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How AI is affecting productivity and jobs in Europe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 如何影响欧洲的生产力与就业</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47068320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47068320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study examines AI&#x27;s impact on productivity and jobs in Europe, finding nuanced effects across sectors and worker types rather than uniform displacement. The analysis suggests AI can boost productivity where complementary investments and skills exist but may exacerbate inequalities without policy measures for retraining and distribution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项研究评估了 AI 对欧洲生产力与就业的影响，发现不同部门与劳动力群体受影响各异，而非一致性替代。研究表明在存在配套投资与技能的情况下 AI 可提升生产力，但若无再培训与分配政策，可能会加剧不平等。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cepr.org/sites/default/files/styles/og_image/public/2026-02/AdobeStock_1772033845.jpeg?itok=jAPHpqwL,https://cepr.org/themes/nhsc-shared/nhsc_base/assets/img/default-social-share.png' data-full='https://cepr.org/sites/default/files/styles/og_image/public/2026-02/AdobeStock_1772033845.jpeg?itok=jAPHpqwL,https://cepr.org/themes/nhsc-shared/nhsc_base/assets/img/default-social-share.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
