<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47046858' data-tags='Energy,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wiki.xxiivv.com/site/permacomputing.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Permacomputing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>永续计算</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47046858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 162</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47046858' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Permacomputing blends permaculture principles with computing to design sustainable tech ecosystems. It advocates energy-efficient hardware, distributed resilience, and open, community-driven maintenance to reduce e-waste and long-term costs. The field encourages critiquing growth-centric tech culture and exploring less resource-intensive deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>永续计算将永续耕作的原则应用到计算领域，旨在设计更可持续的技术生态。它提倡使用节能硬件、分布式韧性以及开放、由社区推动的维护模式，以减少电子垃圾和长期成本。这一理念也在批判以增长为导向的科技文化，探讨资源占用更低的部署方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wiki.xxiivv.com/media/services/rss.jpg' data-full='https://wiki.xxiivv.com/media/services/rss.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47069650' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blackboard.sh/blog/electrobun-v1/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Electrobun v1: Build fast, tiny, and cross-platform desktop apps with TypeScript</a></p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/MeshnetCapital/status/2023573563559547180' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A sitting US president launched two memecoins that wiped out $4.3B+</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>现任美国总统推出两种抹去 43 亿美元的迷因币</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47050300' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47050300' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A claim circulated that a sitting US president launched two memecoins which subsequently collapsed, wiping out over $4.3 billion; if true, it showcases how celebrity or political association can massively inflate speculative token markets. Such incidents highlight the fragility of memecoins and the outsized impact of endorsements or high-profile involvement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有说法称一位现任美国总统推出了两种迷因币，之后崩盘导致超过 43 亿美元蒸发；若属实，这显示了名人或政治人物关联如何能大幅推高投机性代币市场。此类事件凸显迷因币的脆弱性以及名人背书或高调参与的巨大影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HBUtWRNbcAAW6qT.jpg:large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HBUtWRNbcAAW6qT.jpg:large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://girl.surgery/chess' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chess engines do weird stuff</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>象棋引擎会做奇怪的事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 152</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049845' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of bizarre behaviors from chess engines shows they sometimes prefer counterintuitive moves due to evaluation quirks, horizon effects, or specific search heuristics. Understanding these artifacts helps players and developers interpret engine recommendations and improve both tuning and human–engine collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对棋类引擎怪异行为的探讨表明，它们有时会因评估奇异、视野效应或搜索启发式而偏好反直觉走法。了解这些现象有助于棋手与开发者更好解读引擎建议，并改进调参与人机协作。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A minimal x86 kernel written in Zig provides a compact starting point for OS experimentation, demonstrating Zig&#x27;s applicability beyond userland. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个用 Zig 编写的最小化 x86 内核为操作系统实验提供了紧凑的起点，展示了 Zig 在用户态之外的适用性。此类项目对想在现代语言中探索引导、内存管理和硬件初始化的低层程序员很有价值。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/peterjliu/status/2024901585806225723' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Uncovering insiders and alpha on Polymarket with AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 AI 揭示 Polymarket 的内部人士与投资信号</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47091557' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47091557' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 142</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-powered analysis uncovers insider activity and potential alpha signals on Polymarket, illustrating how AI can reveal opaque market dynamics. It notes ethical questions around surveillance and market manipulation risk.</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A court ordered Tesla to pay $243 million in a high-profile Autopilot crash case, highlighting ongoing regulatory scrutiny of autonomous driving systems. The ruling underscores the legal risks automakers face as driver-assistance features mature.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/coccinelle/coccinelle' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Coccinelle: Source-to-source transformation tool</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47098669' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47098669' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Coccinelle is a framework for automating source-to-source transformations in codebases, enabling large-scale refactors and bug fixes with pattern matching and semantic-preserving rewrites. It aids maintainers in applying consistent changes across projects.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Coccinelle 是一个用于在代码库中实现源代码级转换的框架，能够通过模式匹配和语义保持的重写实现大规模重构与修复。有助于维护者在项目中应用一致的改动。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nesbitt.io/2026/02/05/git-magic-files.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Git&#x27;s Magic Files</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Git 的魔法文件</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47111218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 123</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47111218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article digs into Git&#x27;s lesser-known magic files—configurations and attributes that alter how Git behaves beyond ordinary commands. It explains how files like .gitattributes and .gitignore influence merging, pathspecs, and cross-repo tooling. The takeaway is that small, well-chosen magic files can dramatically simplify repository hygiene and collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章梳理了 Git 中鲜为人知的神奇/魔法文件及其对行为的影响。它解释了 .gitattributes、.gitignore 等文件如何影响合并、路径模式以及跨仓工具。小而巧妙的“魔法文件”可以显著简化仓库治理与协作。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/a-e-k/canvas_ity' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Canvas_ity: A tiny, single-header &lt;canvas&gt;-like 2D rasterizer for C++</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Canvas_ity：一个极小、单头文件的 C++ 2D 光栅化库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47103506' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47103506' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 43</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Canvas_ity is a tiny, single-header 2D rasterizer for C++, offering a Canvas-like API with minimal dependencies. It&#x27;s suitable for tiny demos, educational projects, or embedding in tools where keeping the build simple matters.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Canvas_ity 是一个极小的、单头文件的 C++ 2D 光栅化库，提供类似 Canvas 的接口且依赖极少。非常适合小型演示、教学项目或需要简单构建的新工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/879e1d49a48fc25627029190f08aa9a5a1b9e8476be6d585440411edfdca35a9/a-e-k/canvas_ity' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/879e1d49a48fc25627029190f08aa9a5a1b9e8476be6d585440411edfdca35a9/a-e-k/canvas_ity' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47082548' data-tags='Hardware,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sour.coffee/2026/02/20/an-arm-homelab-server-or-a-minisforum-ms-r1-review/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An ARM Homelab Server, or a Minisforum MS-R1 Review</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ARM 家用实验室服务器：Minisforum MS-R1 评测</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47082548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47082548' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 89</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Compact ARM-powered hardware makes a compelling homelab option for lightweight workloads and Docker services. The review puts Minisforum MS-R1 through practical tests of storage expansion, RAM upgrade paths, cooling, and virtualization capabilities, weighing power use and noise against performance. It ultimately argues that while affordable and quiet, the ARM ecosystem and limited expansion may constrain more ambitious lab setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>紧凑的 ARM 硬件为轻量工作负载和容器服务提供了有吸引力的家用实验室解决方案。评测对 Minisforum MS-R1 的存储扩展、内存升级、散热与虚拟化能力进行了实际测试，权衡功耗与噪音与性能。结论是价格友好、运行安静，但 ARM 生态与扩展性可能限制更大规模的实验室需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sour.coffee/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/PXL_20260219_201531792-1024x771.jpg' data-full='https://sour.coffee/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/PXL_20260219_201531792-1024x771.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.yahoo.com/news/articles/u-spent-30-billion-ditch-110200869.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>$30B for laptops yielded a generation less cognitively capable than parents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>$30B 投入笔记本电脑却让新一代认知能力不及父母</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47117087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47117087' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>US education spending on laptops and devices totals around $30B, yet a study suggests this investment did not yield expected gains in cognitive outcomes compared with the parental generation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国在学校使用笔记本和平板电脑的投入约 300 亿美元，但相关研究显示新一代的认知能力并未显著优于父母这一代，引发对教育科技有效性的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(206) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://warnfirehose.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: WARN Firehose – Every US layoff notice in one searchable database</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WARN Firehose：将所有美国裁员通知汇总为一个可检索数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47116026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 114</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47116026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 11</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WARN Firehose aggregates every U.S. layoff notice into a single searchable database, enabling rapid labor-market analysis. It consolidates public WARN notices across states, turning scattered data into a usable resource for researchers, journalists, and workers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>WARN Firehose 将所有美国产裁员通知汇总成一个可检索的数据库，便于快速分析劳动力市场波动。它整合各州的 WARN 通知，帮助研究者、记者和求职者将分散数据转化为可操作的洞察。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47086319' data-tags='Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/02/20/three-engineers-charged-stealing-google-trade-secrets-data-iran-soc-snapdragon.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Silicon Valley engineers were indicted for allegedly sending secrets to Iran</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>硅谷工程师被起诉，涉嫌向伊朗传送机密</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47086319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47086319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Three Silicon Valley engineers were charged over allegedly sending secrets to Iran, underscoring ongoing insider-risk and export-control challenges for tech firms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>三名硅谷工程师被起诉，涉嫌将机密信息发送至伊朗，突显科技公司在内部风险与出口管控方面的持续挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://inputlag.science' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Inputlag.science – Repository of knowledge about input lag in gaming</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47103945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47103945' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This repository aggregates knowledge about gaming input lag, covering measurement methods, testing setups, and practical guidance to reduce latency for both hardware and software. The collection helps developers, testers, and competitive gamers understand and improve perceived responsiveness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该知识库汇总了游戏输入延迟的测量方法、测试流程，以及降低延迟的实用建议，涵盖硬件与软件多方面。为开发者、测试人员和竞技玩家提供理解并提升响应性的参考。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Show HN project demonstrates 3D Mahjong rendered with pure CSS, showcasing how far CSS transforms and perspective can push browser rendering without heavy WebGL. <p class='hn-title'>(221) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.trailofbits.com/2026/02/18/carelessness-versus-craftsmanship-in-cryptography/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Carelessness versus craftsmanship in cryptography</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>密码学中的粗心与匠心</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47060334' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 90</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47060334' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post contrasts sloppy cryptography with craftsmanship, arguing that secure designs come from disciplined engineering rather than quick fixes. It outlines best practices from threat modeling to rigorous verification to mitigate real-world risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章对比了粗心的密码学实践与匠心之作，强调安全设计来自周密的工程实践而非临时修补。提出从威胁建模到严格验证等一系列最佳实践以降低现实风险。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47108977' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 88</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47108977' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocates evidence-based strategies such as immersion, spaced repetition, deliberate practice, and meaningful context. Emphasizes setting clear goals, staying consistent, and using real-world communication to accelerate mastery.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>倡导基于证据的学习策略，如沉浸式练习、间隔重复、刻意练习和有意义的语境。强调设定明确目标、保持持续性，并通过真实沟通来加速掌握。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linuxulator provides Linux ABI compatibility on FreeBSD, letting Linux binaries run on FreeBSD almost seamlessly. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linuxulator 为 FreeBSD 提供 Linux 二进制接口兼容性，使常见 Linux 应用几乎无缝在 FreeBSD 上运行。文中分享了令人惊喜的易用性与性能表现，凸显 FreeBSD 在寻求 Linux 兼容性开发者中的持续价值。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(235) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://zknill.io/posts/chatbots-worst-enemy-is-page-refresh/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A chatbot&#x27;s worst enemy is page refresh</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>聊天机器人最怕页面刷新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47046486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47046486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Page refresh can wipe out chatbot context, breaking continuity in conversations. The piece argues for robust session persistence, local storage of dialogue state, and resilient UX patterns to maintain context across reloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>页面刷新会使聊天机器人丢失对话上下文，打断对话连贯性。文章主张加强会话持久化、本地存储对话状态，并采用对重载更具韧性的用户体验设计。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47035698' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cormullion.github.io/Lindenmayer.jl/stable/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lindenmayer.jl: Defining recursive patterns in Julia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Lindenmayer.jl：在 Julia 中定义递归模式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47035698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47035698' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lindenmayer.jl exposes a compact DSL for defining L-systems in Julia, letting you encode recursive rewriting rules and render fractal-like patterns with minimal boilerplate. The package emphasizes readable syntax and integrates with Julia&#x27;s plotting and numerical ecosystem, enabling quick experimentation with procedural growth and generative art. A handy tool for researchers and developers exploring recursive patterns or graphics in Julia.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lindenmayer.jl 为在 Julia 中定义 L-系统提供了简洁的 DSL，使你能够用最少的样板代码表达递归重写规则并生成分形样式的图案。该包注重可读性语法，并可与 Julia 的绘图和数值计算生态无缝协同，便于快速尝试过程性生长和生成式艺术。对于在 Julia 中探索递归模式或可视化图形的研究者与开发者来说，是一款实用工具。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cormullion.github.io/Lindenmayer.jl/stable/assets/logo.svg' data-full='https://cormullion.github.io/Lindenmayer.jl/stable/assets/logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(237) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-02-scientists-tectonic-moon.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientists discover recent tectonic activity on the moon</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学家在月球上发现最近的地质构造活动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47051709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 65</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47051709' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers report evidence of recent tectonic activity on the Moon, suggesting it&#x27;s geologically active on human timescales. The finding challenges the idea of a completely dormant Moon and has implications for future missions and surface operations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者发现月球存在最近的构造活动，表明月球在人的时间尺度内仍具地质活动。这个发现挑战了月球长期处于休眠状态的观点，并影响未来阿尔忒弥斯计划及月面任务的规划。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(241) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fast KV Compaction via Attention Matching</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper proposes using attention matching to speed up compaction in key-value stores, reducing write amplification and improving throughput. If practical, it could influence how high-throughput databases design compaction pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文提出通过注意力匹配来加速 KV 存储的压缩整理，降低写放大并提升吞吐量。若落地，可能影响高吞吐数据库对压缩整理流水线的设计。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(246) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://keybeekeyboard.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Keybee: A Keyboard Designed for Smartphones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Keybee：专为智能手机设计的键盘</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47074321' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47074321' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Keybee is a smartphone-focused keyboard offering a compact, ergonomic typing experience designed for mobile use.</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在缺乏传统 BlackBerry 键盘的世界里，部分用户与设备正在重新回归物理键盘，以提高打字准确性与触感反馈。文章探讨了适用场景、企业设备以及与触控屏键盘的取舍。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(260) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Leading-AI-IO/palantir-ontology-strategy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Palantir&#x27;s secret weapon isn&#x27;t AI – it&#x27;s Ontology. An open-source deep dive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>帕兰蒂尔的秘密武器不是 AI，而是本体论：开源深度解析</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47107512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47107512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Palantir&#x27;s strategy hinges on ontology—a shared semantic model that unifies data across silos. The open-source dive reveals how ontology-centric design enables faster data product deployment and scalable analytics beyond AI hype. This matters for teams building data platforms that must scale and comply.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>帕兰蒂尔将本体论作为核心策略，借由统一数据的共享语义模型实现跨域分析。开源深入解析揭示了以本体为中心的设计如何加速数据产品落地并提升可扩展性。对于需要跨系统与合规约束的数据平台团队，这一思路具有现实意义。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://signatory.app/#famous-signatures' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Famous Signatures Through History</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>历史上的著名签名</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47073684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47073684' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Take a look at historically famous signatures from rulers, authors, and innovators. The strokes, flourishes, and seals tell stories about authenticity, status, and the era&#x27;s signing habits, offering quick lessons in document forensics and provenance. In a digital age, it contrasts with cryptographic signatures that aim to guarantee integrity and non-repudiation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这组历史著名签名覆盖了各领域的统治者、作者与创新者。笔势、花饰和印章揭示签署的真实性、地位与时代风格，提供文献鉴定和出处追溯的直观案例。相比之下，当下数字签名与密码学证明追求的是同样的可信性，但以全新机制实现。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Symbolic execution is used to find forall-exists bugs, showing how formal methods can automate deep checks over quantified conditions. The work advances software reliability in areas like compilers and verifiers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者用符号执行来发现 forall–exists 的缺陷，展示了形式化方法在对量化条件进行深度检查方面的自动化能力。此类工作提升了编译器与验证工具等领域的软件可靠性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Compact, dependency-free C99 library that implements core music theory concepts: notes, intervals, scales, and chords. It emphasizes a small footprint and easy embedding for projects like games or demos, offering a lightweight alternative to heavier DSP or music libraries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这个极小、无依赖的 C99 音乐理论库实现了核心音乐理论要素，如音符、区间、音阶和和弦。旨在便于嵌入到小游戏或演示类项目，提供一个轻量的替代方案，免去对大型库的依赖。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(265) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ams.org/journals/notices/202603/noti3305/noti3305.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Four-Color Theorem 1852–1976</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>四色定理：1852–1976 的历程</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47100190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47100190' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article traces the four-color theorem from its 19th-century conjecture to the mid-20th-century computer-assisted proof. It demonstrates how collaboration between mathematicians and computers changed the way we validate proofs. The story offers a lens on the history of computational mathematics and the limits of human-only reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文梳理四色定理从提出到通过计算机辅助证明的历程。数学家与计算机的协作改变了证明的验证方式，也凸显了计算在人类推理中的作用。这是对计算数学史与理论界限的深刻反思。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47091763' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(270) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/articles/cm2r6jqm042o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>KFC, Nando&#x27;s, and others ditch chicken welfare pledge</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>肯德基、Nando’s 等取消鸡肉福利承诺</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47091763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47091763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fast-food chains drop positive welfare pledges amid cost pressures, signaling a real-world challenge to maintaining public animal-welfare commitments in supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在成本压力下，快餐连锁纷纷放弃鸡肉福利承诺，反映出供应链环境下公开承诺的现实性与执行难度。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(275) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.forth.com/swiftforth/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SwiftForth IDE for Windows, Linux, macOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SwiftForth IDE：跨 Windows/Linux/macOS 的 Forth 集成开发环境</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47045194' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47045194' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SwiftForth IDE is now available on Windows, Linux, and macOS, unifying the Forth development experience across desktop platforms. The cross-platform IDE bundles editor, compiler, and debugger, making it easier for hobbyists and embedded developers to work with Forth. As a niche language, uptake depends on community and use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SwiftForth IDE 现已在 Windows、Linux、macOS 上可用，为 Forth 开发者提供统一的编辑、编译与调试体验的跨平台工具链。对挖掘嵌入式和复古计算场景的开发者尤具吸引力，更多取决于社区规模与实际需求。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>随机实验选择能否带来更好的理论？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47074083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47074083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章探讨将随机性引入实验是否能促成更好的理论，结合科学哲学与实验设计。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47049499' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(278) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cppstories.com/2026/shared_mutex/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Understanding Std:Shared_mutex from C++17</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>深入理解 C++17 的 std::shared_mutex</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47049499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>std::shared_mutex lets multiple readers access data concurrently while writers gain exclusive access, enabling more scalable read-heavy code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>std::shared_mutex 允许多个读取者并发访问数据，同时写入者获得独占访问，能显著提高读密集型场景的并发性。文章讲解了其 API（lock_shared、unlock_shared、lock）及实际用法要点，并警示了死锁和性能权衡等注意事项。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.cppstories.com/2026/images/shared_mutex.png' data-full='https://www.cppstories.com/2026/images/shared_mutex.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47098324' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(279) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nubificus.co.uk/blog/etcd/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When etcd crashes, check your disks first</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>etcd 崩溃时先检查磁盘</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47098324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47098324' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that disk issues—corruption, full disks, or I/O bottlenecks—are frequent root causes of etcd outages. It recommends starting diagnostics at the storage layer before blaming the cluster, with practical checks and remediation steps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中指出 etcd 崩溃的常见根因往往来自磁盘层的问题，如数据损坏、磁盘已满或 I/O 瓶颈。建议优先从存储层诊断并给出实际排查与修复步骤。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nubificus.co.uk/media/logo_hu_866fdf07312224c.png' data-full='https://nubificus.co.uk/media/logo_hu_866fdf07312224c.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(280) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ft.com/content/5207928a-13e8-4832-8c6f-2e78740c16c9' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Palantir Captured the UK Ministry of Defence</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47099485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 36</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47099485' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Palantir secures a major contract with the UK Ministry of Defence, underscoring the growing role of private vendors in national security. The deal spurs debate over data sovereignty, vendor lock-in, and the influence of AI-driven decision support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Palantir 获得英国国防部的重要合同，凸显私营企业在国家安全领域的角色，同时引发对数据主权和 AI 辅助决策的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(281) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/volatilityfoundation/volatility3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Volatility: The volatile memory forensic extraction framework</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Volatility：易失性内存取证框架</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47110781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 35</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47110781' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Volatility 3 is a memory-forensics framework for extracting artifacts from RAM dumps. The release highlights Python 3 support, a pluggable architecture, and improved tooling for incident response and evidence preservation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Volatility 3 是一个从 RAM 转储中提取证据的内存取证框架。新版本强调对 Python 3 的支持、可插拔架构，以及用于事件响应和证据留存的改进工具链。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(283) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pnas.org/doi/10.1073/pnas.2523071123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fungicide vinclozin causes disease via germline for 20 generations in rats</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>杀菌剂 vinclozin 通过生殖系造成大鼠多代疾病</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47112252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47112252' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research shows fungicide vinclozin can cause disease through the germline across 20 rat generations, indicating potential transgenerational effects. The findings spur safety and regulatory considerations for chemical exposure and health outcomes. It emphasizes the importance of long-term, multigenerational studies in toxicology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新研究显示杀菌剂 vinclozin 通过生殖系造成大鼠多代疾病，具有潜在的跨代效应。研究结果推动对化学暴露与健康后果的安全性与监管讨论，并强调在毒理学中进行长期、多代研究的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(287) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/dronnix/bwarr' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Black-White Array: fast, ordered and based on with O(log N) memory allocations</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Black-White Array：快速、有序且具 O(log N) 内存分配的数组</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47113782' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces a data structure called Black-White Array with fast operations, guaranteed order, and memory allocations of O(log N). It highlights benefits for real-time systems needing predictable memory behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了一种名为 Black-White Array 的数据结构，具备快速操作、可维护的有序性以及 O(log N) 级别的内存分配。它强调了在对内存行为可预测性有严格要求的实时系统中的优势。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(299) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/Hawaii-Macs-AMDGPU-DC-Fix' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Yet Another Fix Coming for Older AMD GPUs on Linux – Thanks to Valve Developer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>又一修复将解决 Linux 上旧 AMD GPU 的问题——感谢 Valve 开发者</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Valve contributors have helped surface a fix for older AMD GPUs on Linux, addressing DC (display core) and driver issues that hinder hardware acceleration. The patch improves stability and performance for users on Hawaii-era cards and similar hardware. It reflects the ongoing, crowd-sourced momentum behind open-source drivers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>来自 Valve 的贡献者推动了 Linux 上旧 AMD GPU 的修复，解决显示核心等驱动问题。补丁提升 Hawaii 系列显卡在 Linux 下的稳定性与性能。这体现了社区协作对开源显卡驱动持续迭代的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(302) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://opennhp.org/blog/the-internet-is-becoming-a-dark-forest.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Internet Is Becoming a Dark Forest – and AI Is the Hunter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>互联网正变成黑暗森林——AI 是猎手</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47107039' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47107039' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Internet Is Becoming a Dark Forest – and AI Is the Hunter. The piece casts the net as a dark forest where adversaries roam; AI accelerates discovery and exploitation, creating new attack surfaces. It outlines defensive priorities for platforms and users, plus policy considerations to preserve openness while reducing risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章把互联网比作黑暗森林，攻击者在其中游走，AI 提高了搜索、探索和攻击能力，带来新的风险点。提出平台与用户应优先强化防御，并从政策层面平衡开放性与安全。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opennhp.org/assets/images/zero-visibility-architecture.jpeg' data-full='https://opennhp.org/assets/images/zero-visibility-architecture.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(303) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.modular.com/blog/the-claude-c-compiler-what-it-reveals-about-the-future-of-software' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chris Lattner: Claude C Compiler</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chris Lattner：Claude C 编译器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47100468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47100468' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Modular blog post about the Claude C compiler, revealing how AI-enabled language tooling can influence software development, compiler design, and safety guarantees. It suggests a future where AI helps produce more reliable, tractable builds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chris Lattner 讨论 Claude C 编译器及其对软件未来的启示，强调 AI 辅助语言设计、编译器安全性与可验证性的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47098839' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(304) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06176' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Large Language Model Reasoning Failures</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大型语言模型推理失败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47098839' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 23</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47098839' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper surveys common reasoning failure modes in large language models, including hallucinations and brittle step-by-step reasoning. Understanding these weaknesses is essential for safe deployment and for guiding future research into robust AI reasoning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文梳理了大型语言模型推理中的常见失败模式，如幻觉、链式推理的不稳定性以及评估偏差。认识这些问题对安全部署和未来改进推理能力至关重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(305) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/us-news/law/openai-employees-raised-alarms-about-canada-shooting-suspect-months-ago-b585df62' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI Employees Raised Alarms About Canada Shooting Suspect Months Ago</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 员工数月前已对加拿大枪击嫌疑人发出警报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47097091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 22</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47097091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI employees reportedly raised alarms about a Canada shooting suspect months before the incident, highlighting tensions between internal risk signals and public-facing safeguards. The story raises questions about how AI companies monitor safety concerns, escalate issues, and incorporate employee insights into policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，OpenAI 员工数月前就加拿大枪击嫌疑人发出警报，揭示内部风险信号与对外安全措施之间的张力。此事引发关于 AI 公司如何监测安全风险、升级告警、以及将员工意见纳入政策制定的讨论。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(309) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ielp.worldtradelaw.net/2026/01/guest-post-president-trump-cannot-legally-impose-tariffs-using-section-122-of-the-trade-act-of-1974/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. Cannot Legally Impose Tariffs Using Section 122 of the Trade Act of 1974</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国不能依法单方面以1974年贸易法第122条征收关税</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47108538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 21</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47108538' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A legal analysis argues that Section 122 cannot legally empower the President to unilaterally impose tariffs, signaling a constraint on executive trade powers. The piece outlines potential statutory and constitutional hurdles and what that means for future U.S. trade policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法律分析认为第122条无法合法授权总统单方面征收关税，凸显对行政权在贸易领域的约束。本文梳理了可能的法定与宪法障碍，以及对未来美国贸易政策的潜在影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The story warns how spreadsheet-driven processes can doom a business when data quality, versioning, and governance are neglected. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>故事警示若把关键流程交给电子表格，企业可能付出高昂代价。强调数据质量、治理与流程纪律的重要性，提倡升级工具与数据工作流以避免脆弱的决策。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the concept of &#x27;write-only code&#x27;—styles that are easy for machines to generate or optimize but hard for humans to understand. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨了“只写型代码”的概念——那些对机器容易生成或优化，但对人类难以理解的编程风格。文章主张在简洁、清晰与可维护性之间取得平衡，为构建可扩展系统的团队提供教训。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Software Development Lifecycle Is Dead argues traditional SDLC is outdated; teams should embrace continuous delivery, rapid iteration, and cross-functional ownership to keep pace with modern software needs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>“软件开发生命周期已死”认为传统的 SDLC 已不再适用，企业应拥抱持续交付、快速迭代与跨职能协作以跟上现代软件开发节奏。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(322) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Baron-von-Riedesel/JWasm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>JWasm: Masm Compatible Assembler</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>JWasm：与 MASM 兼容的汇编器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47044413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47044413' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JWasm provides MASM-compatible assembly syntax and tooling, enabling Windows-style assembly development under modern toolchains. It helps port legacy code or implement high-performance routines using familiar syntax.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>JWasm 提供与 MASM 语法兼容的汇编器，使在现代工具链上进行 Windows 风格的汇编开发成为可能，便于移植遗留代码或实现高性能例程。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(323) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.filfre.net/2026/02/gabriel-knight-3-blood-of-the-sacred-blood-of-the-damned/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gabriel Knight 3: Blood of the Sacred, Blood of the Damned</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gabriel Knight 3：圣血与诅咒之血</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47098593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47098593' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gabriel Knight 3: Blood of the Sacred, Blood of the Damned looks back at the late-90s adventure game&#x27;s storytelling, puzzle design, and use of FMV to drive immersion. It discusses how the title balanced investigative gameplay with cinematic presentation, and how those choices aged over time. The retrospective also touches on preservation and what modern indie titles can learn from its ambition.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《Gabriel Knight 3：圣血与诅咒之血》回顾了这款90年代末冒险游戏的叙事、谜题设计以及对全动作视频（FMV）的运用以提升沉浸感。它分析了游戏如何在调查推进和电影化呈现之间取得平衡，以及这些选择随着时间的推移如何经受考验。文末还讨论了保存与复兴这一经典的意义，以及现代独立游戏设计可能从中汲取的经验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.filfre.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/4124769-gabriel-knight-3-blood-of-the-sacred-blood-of-the-damned-windows-500x600.jpg' data-full='https://www.filfre.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/02/4124769-gabriel-knight-3-blood-of-the-sacred-blood-of-the-damned-windows-500x600.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(325) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/16856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Excessive token usage in Claude Code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 的代币使用过量问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47096937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47096937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The issue notes that Claude Code consumes tokens at an unusually high rate, increasing cost and latency for users. Potential remedies include prompt optimization, shorter contexts, and efficient routing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道指出 Claude Code 的代币消耗异常偏高，导致使用成本与延迟上升。可能的解决办法包括改进提示设计、缩短上下文、提升路由效率等。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Data Studio presents open-source data notebooks combining data, code, and visuals in a notebook interface. It aims to provide an alternative to proprietary data science tools, emphasizing transparency and reproducibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Data Studio 将数据、代码与可视化整合到一个笔记本界面，提供开源的数据笔记本工具，强调透明性和可重复性，成为专有工具的有力替代。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位客户披露与 AWS 的账单纠纷导致账户被暂停，扣款 1600 美元，数日后仍无人工客服回应。该事件凸显大型云服务商在支持与账单透明度方面的问题。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(337) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/02/21/technology/ai-boom-backlash.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>People Loved the Dot-Com Boom. The A.I. Boom, Not So Much</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人们喜欢互联网时代的繁荣，AI 繁荣却不那么受欢迎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47107819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 14</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47107819' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article argues that public enthusiasm followed the dot-com era, while the current AI boom faces more backlash. It traces why hype hasn&#x27;t translated into durable user value and what founders should do to deliver tangible benefits and responsible deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为互联网泡沫时代曾掀起热潮，而当下的 AI 热潮更易引发反弹。它分析高涨的热度为何尚未转化为长期的用户价值，并就创始人如何提供切实利益和负责任的 AI 部署提出建议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static01.nyt.com/images/2026/02/22/multimedia/21biz-ai-backlash-altman-ckvl/21biz-ai-backlash-altman-ckvl-facebookJumbo.jpg' data-full='https://static01.nyt.com/images/2026/02/22/multimedia/21biz-ai-backlash-altman-ckvl/21biz-ai-backlash-altman-ckvl-facebookJumbo.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(339) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A New Perspective on Drawing Venn Diagrams for Data Visualization</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes a fresh perspective on drawing Venn diagrams for data visualization, addressing readability for multi-set diagrams and comparing alternatives like Euler diagrams.</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47088307' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 13</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47088307' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

It reflects on the social and economic forces that broadened access to toys.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约客评析追溯一群犹太裔企业家如何打造美国玩具产业，考察资本、文化与制造业如何推动大众化玩具市场的形成。文章反思推动玩具普及的社会与经济力量。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47057218' data-tags='Education,Culture,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://henryjenkins.org/blog/2010/04/choose_your_ficitons_well.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Choose Your Fictions Well (2010)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谨慎选择你的虚构</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47057218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 13</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47057218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Henry Jenkins&#x27; 2010 meditation on media literacy argues audiences should critically curate the fictions they trust in a media-saturated landscape. Its insights remain relevant for evaluating AI-generated content and online narratives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亨利·詹金斯 2010 年的媒体素养思考强调读者应谨慎地筛选信任的虚构内容，在信息泛滥的时代保持批判性。该观点对于评估 AI 生成内容以及在线叙事仍具现实意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/592880808419c27d193683ef/t/68f06068e4bbe70af79a9b62/1760583784163/Pop+Junctions+new+logo.png?format=1500w' data-full='http://static1.squarespace.com/static/592880808419c27d193683ef/t/68f06068e4bbe70af79a9b62/1760583784163/Pop+Junctions+new+logo.png?format=1500w' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

It covers datasets, tooling, and evaluation strategies to make verifiers more reliable in real-world code.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft confirms leadership shake at Xbox as Phil Spencer exits and an AI executive takes the helm, signaling a strategic shift toward integrating AI capabilities with gaming. Expect impacts on product roadmap, cloud services, and developer relations.</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>

<p class='hn-title'>(347) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/redruM0381/ATAboy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ATAboy is a USB adapter for legacy CHS only style IDE (PATA) drives</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ATAboy：用于遗留 CHS/IDE（PATA）硬盘的 USB 适配器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47095972' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47095972' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ATAboy is a USB adapter that connects legacy CHS/IDE drives to modern systems, enabling data recovery and access to old disks. It showcases ongoing resilience of older hardware interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ATAboy 是一款 USB 适配器，能够让旧式 CHS/IDE（PATA）硬盘连接到现代系统，便于数据恢复。它体现了早期接口在现代环境中的持续可用性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你想打造一个以搜索还是停留为核心的社区吗？（2021）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47048181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47048181' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Examines the trade-offs between building a community around searchable content versus user engagement and hangout culture, drawing lessons from 2021 to guide product design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨在社区产品中：是以可搜索内容为核心，还是以用户互动与停留为核心；结合 2021 年的经验给出设计与运营的启示。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47097904' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(349) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.biometricupdate.com/202602/colorado-moves-age-checks-from-websites-to-operating-systems' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Colorado moves age checks from websites to operating systems</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科罗拉多将年龄验证从网站迁移至操作系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47097904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 10</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47097904' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Colorado moves age checks to OS level, enabling devices to prove user age without sites implementing verification themselves. This could streamline compliance and enhance privacy, but raises concerns about OS market power, accessibility, and how trust is established across apps and browsers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科州计划把年龄验证放到设备操作系统层来处理，让应用不再需要在每个网站单独验证年龄。此举或简化合规并提升隐私，但也引发对操作系统垄断、无障碍性以及跨应用信任机制的担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d1sr9z1pdl3mb7.cloudfront.net/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/14100643/teens-phones-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://d1sr9z1pdl3mb7.cloudfront.net/wp-content/uploads/2025/02/14100643/teens-phones-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(350) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/psyb0t/piraterf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: PIrateRF – Turn a $20 Raspberry Pi Zero into a 12-mode RF transmitter</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PIrateRF：用 20 美元的 Raspberry Pi Zero 实现 12 模式射频发射</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47047559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 9</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47047559' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>PIrateRF shows how a $20 Raspberry Pi Zero can drive a 12-mode RF transmitter, turning a cheap board into a versatile RF project. It demonstrates the feasibility of hobbyist-grade hardware for experimenting with radio signals and modulation schemes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PIrateRF 展示了用 20 美元的 Raspberry Pi Zero 驱动一个具备 12 种模式的射频发射器，体现了廉价开发板在无线信号与调制方案实验中的可行性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(351) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47096131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Agent Passport – OAuth-like identity verification for AI agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Agent Passport——AI 代理的 OAuth 式身份验证</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47096131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47096131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Agent Passport provides an OAuth-like identity framework for AI agents, enabling secure authentication and cross-tool collaboration. It helps prevent impersonation and improves auditability in multi-agent setups.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Agent Passport 提供类似 OAuth 的 AI 代理身份验证框架，支持跨工具协作中的安全认证与授权。提升多代理系统的防伪能力与可审计性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(352) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.modular.com/blog/the-claude-c-compiler-what-it-reveals-about-the-future-of-software' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Claude C Compiler: What It Reveals About the Future of Software</a></p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47058350' data-tags='Politics,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(353) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://clereviewofbooks.com/isabel-jacobs-boris-groys-marco-filoni/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Famous Enigma: On Alexandre Kojève</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>名著之谜：解读 Alexandre Kojève</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47058350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47058350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This exploration of Kojève delves into the French philosopher&#x27;s interpretation of Hegel and his influence on 20th-century political thought, including the reception of his ideas in Cold War-era intellectual circles. It sheds light on how Kojève helped shape debates on history, sovereignty, and liberalism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文解读 Kojève 的思想，聚焦他对黑格尔的解读及其对20世纪政治思想的影响，尤其在冷战时期知识分子圈中的传播与讨论。揭示他如何影响历史观、主权与自由主义的辩论。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(354) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hollisrobbinsanecdotal.substack.com/p/attention-is-all-you-need-to-bankrupt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Attention is all you need to bankrupt a university</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>注意力就是让大学破产的关键</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47107080' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47107080' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay argues attention economy could bankrupt universities, urging schools to rethink pricing, value propositions, and governance amid a distracted audience. It discusses strategic pivots for higher education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章认为注意力经济可能侵蚀高校的收入和生存空间，促使学校重新思考定价、价值定位和治理结构。讨论高等教育的潜在转型路径。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47097554' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(355) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://script-snap.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Script Snap – Extract code from videos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Script Snap—从视频中提取代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47097554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 6</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47097554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Script Snap extracts code shown in videos into editable text, helping developers salvage snippets from tutorials that aren&#x27;t easily copyable. It combines OCR with code-aware parsing to improve accuracy, though success depends on video quality and formatting. This could accelerate learning and reuse of demonstrated techniques.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Script Snap 能将视频中显示的代码提取为可编辑文本，方便从教学视频中回收无法直接复制的代码片段。它结合 OCR 与代码识别，准确性受画面清晰度和排版影响。此工具有助于学习与复用视频中的示例代码。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://script-snap.com/_next/image?url=%2Fassets%2Fvisual-truth-table.png&amp;w=3840&amp;q=75' data-full='https://script-snap.com/_next/image?url=%2Fassets%2Fvisual-truth-table.png&amp;w=3840&amp;q=75' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47084349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 4</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47084349' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A DIY e-ink air traffic monitor updates live aircraft data via Cloudflare Workers, demonstrating edge computing-powered dashboards with minimal power use. It showcases how lightweight web workers can drive practical hardware displays.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个自制的电子纸空中交通监控显示器通过 Cloudflare Workers 实时更新航班数据，演示了基于边缘计算的低功耗仪表板。展示了轻量级 Web 工作流在实际硬件显示中的应用潜力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(357) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fostrom.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Fostrom, an IoT Cloud Platform built for developers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：为开发者打造的物联网云平台 Fostrom</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47084431' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47084431' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fostrom presents a developer-focused IoT cloud platform, likely offering device management, data routes, and edge compatibility with a simple API. This kind of project showcases how lean IoT backends can compete with hyperscale offerings when developer experience is prioritised.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fostrom 是一个面向开发者的物联网云平台，提供设备管理、数据路由和边缘计算兼容等能力，API 设计简洁。此类项目证明了在重视开发者体验时，轻量级物联网后端也能与大厂云方案竞争。</p>
